   373	object DataPlaneAcceptor {
   374	  val ThreadPrefix: String = "data-plane"
   375	  val MetricPrefix: String = ""
   376	  val ListenerReconfigurableConfigs: Set[String] = Set(SocketServerConfigs.NUM_NETWORK_THREADS_CONFIG)
   377	}
   378	
   379	class DataPlaneAcceptor(socketServer: SocketServer,
   380	                        endPoint: EndPoint,
   381	                        config: KafkaConfig,
   382	                        nodeId: Int,
   383	                        connectionQuotas: ConnectionQuotas,
   384	                        time: Time,
   385	                        isPrivilegedListener: Boolean,
   386	                        requestChannel: RequestChannel,
   387	                        metrics: Metrics,
   388	                        credentialProvider: CredentialProvider,
   389	                        logContext: LogContext,
   390	                        memoryPool: MemoryPool,
   391	                        apiVersionManager: ApiVersionManager)
   392	  extends Acceptor(socketServer,
   393	                   endPoint,
   394	                   config,
   395	                   nodeId,
   396	                   connectionQuotas,
   397	                   time,
   398	                   isPrivilegedListener,
   399	                   requestChannel,
   400	                   metrics,
   401	                   credentialProvider,
   402	                   logContext,
   403	                   memoryPool,
   404	                   apiVersionManager) with ListenerReconfigurable {
   405	
   406	  override def metricPrefix(): String = DataPlaneAcceptor.MetricPrefix
   407	  override def threadPrefix(): String = DataPlaneAcceptor.ThreadPrefix
   408	
   409	  /**
   410	   * Returns the listener name associated with this reconfigurable. Listener-specific
   411	   * configs corresponding to this listener name are provided for reconfiguration.
   412	   */
   413	  override def listenerName(): ListenerName = endPoint.listenerName
   414	
   415	  /**
   416	   * Returns the names of configs that may be reconfigured.
   417	   */
   418	  override def reconfigurableConfigs(): util.Set[String] = DataPlaneAcceptor.ListenerReconfigurableConfigs.asJava
   419	
   420	
   421	  /**
   422	   * Validates the provided configuration. The provided map contains
   423	   * all configs including any reconfigurable configs that may be different
   424	   * from the initial configuration. Reconfiguration will be not performed
   425	   * if this method throws any exception.
   426	   *
   427	   * @throws ConfigException if the provided configs are not valid. The exception
   428	   *                         message from ConfigException will be returned to the client in
   429	   *                         the AlterConfigs response.
   430	   */
   431	  override def validateReconfiguration(configs: util.Map[String, _]): Unit = {
   432	    configs.forEach { (k, v) =>
   433	      if (reconfigurableConfigs().contains(k)) {
   434	        val newValue = v.asInstanceOf[Int]
   435	        val oldValue = processors.length
   436	        if (newValue != oldValue) {
   437	          val errorMsg = s"Dynamic thread count update validation failed for $k=$v"
   438	          if (newValue <= 0)
   439	            throw new ConfigException(s"$errorMsg, value should be at least 1")
   440	          if (newValue < oldValue / 2)
   441	            throw new ConfigException(s"$errorMsg, value should be at least half the current value $oldValue")
   442	          if (newValue > oldValue * 2)
   443	            throw new ConfigException(s"$errorMsg, value should not be greater than double the current value $oldValue")
   444	        }
   445	      }
   446	    }
   447	  }
   448	
   449	  /**
   450	   * Reconfigures this instance with the given key-value pairs. The provided
   451	   * map contains all configs including any reconfigurable configs that
   452	   * may have changed since the object was initially configured using
   453	   * [[org.apache.kafka.common.Configurable#configure( Map )]]. This method will only be invoked if
   454	   * the configs have passed validation using [[validateReconfiguration( Map )]].
   455	   */
   456	  override def reconfigure(configs: util.Map[String, _]): Unit = {
   457	    val newNumNetworkThreads = configs.get(SocketServerConfigs.NUM_NETWORK_THREADS_CONFIG).asInstanceOf[Int]
   458	
   459	    if (newNumNetworkThreads != processors.length) {
   460	      info(s"Resizing network thread pool size for ${endPoint.listenerName} listener from ${processors.length} to $newNumNetworkThreads")
   461	      if (newNumNetworkThreads > processors.length) {
   462	        addProcessors(newNumNetworkThreads - processors.length)
   463	      } else if (newNumNetworkThreads < processors.length) {
   464	        removeProcessors(processors.length - newNumNetworkThreads)
   465	      }
   466	    }
   467	  }
   468	
   469	  /**
   470	   * Configure this class with the given key-value pairs
   471	   */
   472	  override def configure(configs: util.Map[String, _]): Unit = {
   473	    addProcessors(configs.get(SocketServerConfigs.NUM_NETWORK_THREADS_CONFIG).asInstanceOf[Int])
   474	  }
   475	}
   476	
   477	/**
   478	 * Thread that accepts and configures new connections. There is one of these per endpoint.
   479	 */
   480	private[kafka] abstract class Acceptor(val socketServer: SocketServer,
   481	                                       val endPoint: EndPoint,
   482	                                       var config: KafkaConfig,
   483	                                       nodeId: Int,
   484	                                       val connectionQuotas: ConnectionQuotas,
   485	                                       time: Time,
   486	                                       isPrivilegedListener: Boolean,
   487	                                       requestChannel: RequestChannel,
   488	                                       metrics: Metrics,
   489	                                       credentialProvider: CredentialProvider,
   490	                                       logContext: LogContext,
   491	                                       memoryPool: MemoryPool,
   492	                                       apiVersionManager: ApiVersionManager)
   493	  extends Runnable with Logging {
   494	
   495	  private val metricsGroup = new KafkaMetricsGroup(this.getClass)
   496	
   497	  val shouldRun = new AtomicBoolean(true)
   498	
   499	  def metricPrefix(): String
   500	  def threadPrefix(): String
   501	
   502	  private val sendBufferSize = config.socketSendBufferBytes
   503	  private val recvBufferSize = config.socketReceiveBufferBytes
   504	  private val listenBacklogSize = config.socketListenBacklogSize
   505	
   506	  private val nioSelector = NSelector.open()
   507	
   508	  // If the port is configured as 0, we are using a wildcard port, so we need to open the socket
   509	  // before we can find out what port we have. If it is set to a nonzero value, defer opening
   510	  // the socket until we start the Acceptor. The reason for deferring the socket opening is so
   511	  // that systems which assume that the socket being open indicates readiness are not confused.
   512	  private[network] var serverChannel: ServerSocketChannel  = _
   513	  private[network] val localPort: Int  = if (endPoint.port != 0) {
   514	    endPoint.port
   515	  } else {
   516	    serverChannel = openServerSocket(endPoint.host, endPoint.port, listenBacklogSize)
   517	    val newPort = serverChannel.socket().getLocalPort
   518	    info(s"Opened wildcard endpoint ${endPoint.host}:$newPort")
   519	    newPort
   520	  }
   521	
   522	  private[network] val processors = new ArrayBuffer[Processor]()
   523	  // Build the metric name explicitly in order to keep the existing name for compatibility
   524	  private val backwardCompatibilityMetricGroup = new KafkaMetricsGroup("kafka.network", "Acceptor")
   525	  private val blockedPercentMeterMetricName = backwardCompatibilityMetricGroup.metricName(
   526	    s"${metricPrefix()}AcceptorBlockedPercent",
   527	    Map(ListenerMetricTag -> endPoint.listenerName.value).asJava)
   528	  private val blockedPercentMeter = metricsGroup.newMeter(blockedPercentMeterMetricName,"blocked time", TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS)
   529	  // Exposed so Processor.applyConnectionQuotasForNewlyAcceptedChannels can call
   530	  // ConnectionQuotas.inc on the io_uring accept path (where the Acceptor doesn't run
   531	  // inc(), because Netty's event loop handed the channel straight to the Processor).
   532	  private[network] def acceptorBlockedPercentMeter: com.yammer.metrics.core.Meter = blockedPercentMeter
   533	  private var currentProcessorIndex = 0
   534	  private[network] val throttledSockets = new mutable.PriorityQueue[DelayedCloseSocket]()
   535	  private val started = new AtomicBoolean()
   536	  private[network] val startedFuture = new CompletableFuture[Void]()
   537	
   538	  val thread: KafkaThread = KafkaThread.nonDaemon(
   539	    s"${threadPrefix()}-kafka-socket-acceptor-${endPoint.listenerName}-${endPoint.securityProtocol}-${endPoint.port}",
   540	    this)
   541	
   542	  def start(): Unit = synchronized {
   543	    try {
   544	      if (!shouldRun.get()) {
   545	        throw new ClosedChannelException()
   546	      }
   547	      // io_uring listeners bind via the per-Processor IoUringServerListener (SO_REUSEPORT),
   548	      // so the Acceptor must NOT open a NIO ServerSocketChannel on the same address — doing
   549	      // so would either fail with EADDRINUSE (if the Processor bound first) or steal accepts
   550	      // away from the io_uring path. The Acceptor thread still starts, but its run loop
   551	      // skips the NIO accept work for io_uring listeners.
   552	      if (serverChannel == null && !usesIoUring) {
   553	        serverChannel = openServerSocket(endPoint.host, endPoint.port, listenBacklogSize)
   554	        debug(s"Opened endpoint ${endPoint.host}:${endPoint.port}")
   555	      }
   556	      debug(s"Starting processors for listener ${endPoint.listenerName}")
   557	      processors.foreach(_.start())
   558	      debug(s"Starting acceptor thread for listener ${endPoint.listenerName}")
   559	      thread.start()
   560	      startedFuture.complete(null)
   561	      started.set(true)
   562	    } catch {
   563	      case e: ClosedChannelException =>
   564	        debug(s"Refusing to start acceptor for ${endPoint.listenerName} since the acceptor has already been shut down.")
   565	        startedFuture.completeExceptionally(e)
   566	      case t: Throwable =>
   567	        error(s"Unable to start acceptor for ${endPoint.listenerName}", t)
   568	        startedFuture.completeExceptionally(new RuntimeException(s"Unable to start acceptor for ${endPoint.listenerName}", t))
   569	    }
   570	  }
   571	
   572	  private[network] case class DelayedCloseSocket(socket: SocketChannel, endThrottleTimeMs: Long) extends Ordered[DelayedCloseSocket] {
   573	    override def compare(that: DelayedCloseSocket): Int = endThrottleTimeMs compare that.endThrottleTimeMs
   574	  }
   575	
   576	  private[network] def removeProcessors(removeCount: Int): Unit = synchronized {
   577	    // Shutdown `removeCount` processors. Remove them from the processor list first so that no more
   578	    // connections are assigned. Shutdown the removed processors, closing the selector and its connections.
   579	    // The processors are then removed from `requestChannel` and any pending responses to these processors are dropped.
   580	    val toRemove = processors.takeRight(removeCount)
   581	    processors.remove(processors.size - removeCount, removeCount)
   582	    toRemove.foreach(_.close())
   583	    toRemove.foreach(processor => requestChannel.removeProcessor(processor.id))
   584	  }
   585	
   586	  def beginShutdown(): Unit = {
   587	    if (shouldRun.getAndSet(false)) {
   588	      wakeup()
   589	      synchronized {
   590	        processors.foreach(_.beginShutdown())
   591	      }
   592	    }
   593	  }
   594	
   595	  def close(): Unit = {
   596	    beginShutdown()
   597	    thread.join()
   598	    if (!started.get) {
   599	      closeAll()
   600	    }
   601	    synchronized {
   602	      processors.foreach(_.close())
   603	    }
   604	  }
   605	
   606	  /**
   607	   * Accept loop that checks for new connection attempts. For io_uring listeners the per-
   608	   * Processor IoUringServerListener owns the accept loop via Netty, so the Acceptor thread
   609	   * has nothing to dispatch — it just blocks on the nioSelector with a timeout so wakeup()
   610	   * during shutdown still returns promptly.
   611	   */
   612	  override def run(): Unit = {
   613	    val ioUring = usesIoUring
   614	    if (!ioUring) {
   615	      serverChannel.register(nioSelector, SelectionKey.OP_ACCEPT)
   616	    }
   617	    try {
   618	      while (shouldRun.get()) {
   619	        try {
   620	          if (ioUring) {
   621	            nioSelector.select(500)
   622	            nioSelector.selectedKeys().clear()
   623	          } else {
   624	            acceptNewConnections()
   625	          }
   626	          closeThrottledConnections()
   627	        }
   628	        catch {
   629	          // We catch all the throwables to prevent the acceptor thread from exiting on exceptions due
   630	          // to a select operation on a specific channel or a bad request. We don't want
   631	          // the broker to stop responding to requests from other clients in these scenarios.
   632	          case e: ControlThrowable => throw e
   633	          case e: Throwable => error("Error occurred", e)
   634	        }
   635	      }
   636	    } finally {
   637	      closeAll()
   638	    }
   639	  }
   640	
   641	  /** Whether this Acceptor's listener is served by the io_uring backend. */
   642	  private[network] def usesIoUring: Boolean =
   643	    config.usesIoUring(endPoint.securityProtocol)
   644	
   645	  private def closeAll(): Unit = {
   646	    debug("Closing server socket, selector, and any throttled sockets.")
   647	    // The serverChannel will be null if Acceptor's thread is not started
   648	    Utils.closeQuietly(serverChannel, "Acceptor serverChannel")
   649	    Utils.closeQuietly(nioSelector, "Acceptor nioSelector")
   650	    throttledSockets.foreach(throttledSocket => closeSocket(throttledSocket.socket))
   651	    throttledSockets.clear()
   652	  }
   653	
   654	  /**
   655	   * Create a server socket to listen for connections on.
   656	   */
   657	  private def openServerSocket(host: String, port: Int, listenBacklogSize: Int): ServerSocketChannel = {
   658	    val socketAddress = if (Utils.isBlank(host)) {
   659	      new InetSocketAddress(port)
   660	    } else {
   661	      new InetSocketAddress(host, port)
   662	    }
   663	    val serverChannel = socketServer.socketFactory.openServerSocket(
   664	      endPoint.listenerName.value(),
   665	      socketAddress,
   666	      listenBacklogSize,
   667	      recvBufferSize)
   668	    info(s"Awaiting socket connections on ${socketAddress.getHostString}:${serverChannel.socket.getLocalPort}.")
   669	    serverChannel
   670	  }
   671	
   672	  /**
   673	   * Listen for new connections and assign accepted connections to processors using round-robin.
   674	   */
   675	  private def acceptNewConnections(): Unit = {
   676	    val ready = nioSelector.select(500)
   677	    if (ready > 0) {
   678	      val keys = nioSelector.selectedKeys()
   679	      val iter = keys.iterator()
   680	      while (iter.hasNext && shouldRun.get()) {
   681	        try {
   682	          val key = iter.next
   683	          iter.remove()
   684	
   685	          if (key.isAcceptable) {
   686	            accept(key).foreach { socketChannel =>
   687	              // Assign the channel to the next processor (using round-robin) to which the
   688	              // channel can be added without blocking. If newConnections queue is full on
   689	              // all processors, block until the last one is able to accept a connection.
   690	              var retriesLeft = synchronized(processors.length)
   691	              var processor: Processor = null
   692	              do {
   693	                retriesLeft -= 1
   694	                processor = synchronized {
   695	                  // adjust the index (if necessary) and retrieve the processor atomically for
   696	                  // correct behaviour in case the number of processors is reduced dynamically
   697	                  currentProcessorIndex = currentProcessorIndex % processors.length
   698	                  processors(currentProcessorIndex)
   699	                }
   700	                currentProcessorIndex += 1
   701	              } while (!assignNewConnection(socketChannel, processor, retriesLeft == 0))
   702	            }
   703	          } else
   704	            throw new IllegalStateException("Unrecognized key state for acceptor thread.")
   705	        } catch {
   706	          case e: Throwable => error("Error while accepting connection", e)
   707	        }
   708	      }
   709	    }
   710	  }
   711	
   712	  /**
   713	   * Accept a new connection
   714	   */
   715	  private def accept(key: SelectionKey): Option[SocketChannel] = {
   716	    val serverSocketChannel = key.channel().asInstanceOf[ServerSocketChannel]
   717	    val socketChannel = serverSocketChannel.accept()
   718	    try {
   719	      connectionQuotas.inc(endPoint.listenerName, socketChannel.socket.getInetAddress, blockedPercentMeter)
   720	      configureAcceptedSocketChannel(socketChannel)
   721	      Some(socketChannel)
   722	    } catch {
   723	      case e: TooManyConnectionsException =>
   724	        info(s"Rejected connection from ${e.ip}, address already has the configured maximum of ${e.count} connections.")
   725	        connectionQuotas.closeChannel(this, endPoint.listenerName, socketChannel)
   726	        None
   727	      case e: ConnectionThrottledException =>
   728	        val ip = socketChannel.socket.getInetAddress
   729	        debug(s"Delaying closing of connection from $ip for ${e.throttleTimeMs} ms")
   730	        val endThrottleTimeMs = e.startThrottleTimeMs + e.throttleTimeMs
   731	        throttledSockets += DelayedCloseSocket(socketChannel, endThrottleTimeMs)
   732	        None
   733	      case e: IOException =>
   734	        error(s"Encountered an error while configuring the connection, closing it.", e)
   735	        connectionQuotas.closeChannel(this, endPoint.listenerName, socketChannel)
   736	        None
   737	    }
   738	  }
   739	
   740	  protected def configureAcceptedSocketChannel(socketChannel: SocketChannel): Unit = {
   741	    socketChannel.configureBlocking(false)
   742	    socketChannel.socket().setTcpNoDelay(true)
   743	    socketChannel.socket().setKeepAlive(true)
   744	    if (sendBufferSize != Selectable.USE_DEFAULT_BUFFER_SIZE)
   745	      socketChannel.socket().setSendBufferSize(sendBufferSize)
   746	  }
   747	
   748	  /**
   749	   * Close sockets for any connections that have been throttled.
   750	   */
   751	  private def closeThrottledConnections(): Unit = {
   752	    val timeMs = time.milliseconds
   753	    while (throttledSockets.headOption.exists(_.endThrottleTimeMs < timeMs)) {
   754	      val closingSocket = throttledSockets.dequeue()
   755	      debug(s"Closing socket from ip ${closingSocket.socket.getRemoteAddress}")
   756	      closeSocket(closingSocket.socket)
   757	    }
   758	  }
   759	
   760	  private def assignNewConnection(socketChannel: SocketChannel, processor: Processor, mayBlock: Boolean): Boolean = {
   761	    if (processor.accept(socketChannel, mayBlock, blockedPercentMeter)) {
   762	      debug(s"Accepted connection from ${socketChannel.socket.getRemoteSocketAddress} on" +
   763	        s" ${socketChannel.socket.getLocalSocketAddress} and assigned it to processor ${processor.id}," +
   764	        s" sendBufferSize [actual|requested]: [${socketChannel.socket.getSendBufferSize}|$sendBufferSize]" +
   765	        s" recvBufferSize [actual|requested]: [${socketChannel.socket.getReceiveBufferSize}|$recvBufferSize]")
   766	      true
   767	    } else
   768	      false
   769	  }
   770	
   771	  /**
   772	   * Wakeup the thread for selection.
   773	   */
   774	  def wakeup(): Unit = nioSelector.wakeup()
   775	
   776	  def addProcessors(toCreate: Int): Unit = synchronized {
   777	    val listenerName = endPoint.listenerName
   778	    val securityProtocol = endPoint.securityProtocol
   779	    val listenerProcessors = new ArrayBuffer[Processor]()
   780	
   781	    for (_ <- 0 until toCreate) {
   782	      val processor = newProcessor(socketServer.nextProcessorId(), listenerName, securityProtocol, socketServer.connectionDisconnectListeners)
   783	      listenerProcessors += processor
   784	      requestChannel.addProcessor(processor)
   785	
   786	      if (started.get) {
   787	        processor.start()
   788	      }
   789	    }

  1262	  private def updateRequestMetrics(response: RequestChannel.Response): Unit = {
  1263	    val request = response.request
  1264	    val networkThreadTimeNanos = openOrClosingChannel(request.context.connectionId).fold(0L)(_.getAndResetNetworkThreadTimeNanos())
  1265	    request.updateRequestMetrics(networkThreadTimeNanos, response)
  1266	  }
  1267	
  1268	  /**
  1269	   * Apply ConnectionQuotas.inc for channels that just surfaced from selector.connected().
  1270	   * io_uring listeners accept inside Netty's event loop and never went through
  1271	   * Acceptor.accept (which is where NIO inc'd the quota), so the gate has to land here
  1272	   * instead.
  1273	   *
  1274	   * Refusal paths:
  1275	   *  - tryInc returns false (broker max-connections hit, or listener connection-rate
  1276	   *    throttle): no inc happened, so we just close the selector channel.
  1277	   *  - TooManyConnectionsException: tryInc increments the per-IP / listener / total
  1278	   *    counters BEFORE checking the per-IP max (see ConnectionQuotas.tryInc), so by
  1279	   *    the time the exception fires the inc has already landed. selector.close on the
  1280	   *    io_uring path is silent (it does NOT surface in selector.disconnected, so
  1281	   *    processDisconnected will not run the normal dec). Roll the inc back here.
  1282	   *  - ConnectionThrottledException: thrown by recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle, which
  1283	   *    runs before the inc, so no rollback is needed.
  1284	   *
  1285	   * For NIO listeners selector.connected() is always empty (broker-side Processors don't
  1286	   * initiate outgoing connections), so this method is a hot-path no-op there.
  1287	   */
  1288	  private def applyConnectionQuotasForNewlyAcceptedChannels(): Unit = {
  1289	    val newlyConnected = selector.connected()
  1290	    if (newlyConnected.isEmpty) return
  1291	    newlyConnected.forEach { connectionId =>
  1292	      val channel = selector.channel(connectionId)
  1293	      if (channel != null) {
  1294	        val address = channel.socketAddress
  1295	        if (address != null) {
  1296	          try {
  1297	            // tryInc — never blocks. On NIO the dedicated Acceptor thread can wait for a
  1298	            // slot via inc(); here the caller is the Processor's poll loop, so a wait
  1299	            // would stall every other channel on this Processor.
  1300	            if (!connectionQuotas.tryInc(listenerName, address)) {
  1301	              info(s"Closing io_uring connection $connectionId from $address: broker- or " +
  1302	                s"listener-level connection slot unavailable or rate-limited.")
  1303	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1304	            }
  1305	          } catch {
  1306	            case e: TooManyConnectionsException =>
  1307	              info(s"Closing io_uring connection $connectionId from ${e.ip}: " +
  1308	                s"already has the configured maximum of ${e.count} connections.")
  1309	              // tryInc incremented counts before throwing; selector.close is silent on
  1310	              // the io_uring path, so processDisconnected will not run dec. Roll back
  1311	              // the inc explicitly, otherwise the per-IP / listener / total counters
  1312	              // leak permanently and the next refusal compares against a stale count.
  1313	              connectionQuotas.dec(listenerName, address)
  1314	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1315	            case e: ConnectionThrottledException =>
  1316	              debug(s"Closing throttled io_uring connection $connectionId from $address " +
  1317	                s"(throttle ${e.throttleTimeMs}ms)")
  1318	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1319	          }
  1320	        }
  1321	      }
  1322	    }
  1323	  }
  1324	
  1325	  private def processDisconnected(): Unit = {
  1326	    selector.disconnected.keySet.forEach { connectionId =>
  1327	      try {
  1328	        val remoteHost = ServerConnectionId.fromString(connectionId).orElseThrow { () =>
  1329	          throw new IllegalStateException(s"connectionId has unexpected format: $connectionId")
  1330	        }.remoteHost
  1331	        inflightResponses.remove(connectionId).foreach(updateRequestMetrics)
  1332	        // the channel has been closed by the selector but the quotas still need to be updated
  1333	        connectionQuotas.dec(listenerName, InetAddress.getByName(remoteHost))
  1334	        // Call listeners to notify for closed connection.
  1335	        connectionDisconnectListeners.foreach(listener => CoreUtils.swallow(() -> listener.onDisconnect(connectionId), this, Level.ERROR))
  1336	      } catch {
  1337	        case e: Throwable => processException(s"Exception while processing disconnection of $connectionId", e)
  1338	      }
  1339	    }
  1340	  }
  1341	
  1342	  private def closeExcessConnections(): Unit = {
  1343	    if (connectionQuotas.maxConnectionsExceeded(listenerName)) {
  1344	      val channel = selector.lowestPriorityChannel()
  1345	      if (channel != null)
  1346	        close(channel.id)
  1347	    }
  1348	  }
  1349	
  1350	  /**
  1351	   * Close the connection identified by `connectionId` and decrement the connection count.
  1352	   * The channel will be immediately removed from the selector's `channels` or `closingChannels`
  1353	   * and no further disconnect notifications will be sent for this channel by the selector.
  1354	   * If responses are pending for the channel, they are dropped and metrics is updated.
  1355	   * If the channel has already been removed from selector, no action is taken.
  1356	   */
  1357	  private def close(connectionId: String): Unit = {
  1358	    openOrClosingChannel(connectionId).foreach { channel =>
  1359	      debug(s"Closing selector connection $connectionId")
  1360	      val address = channel.socketAddress
  1361	      if (address != null)
  1362	        connectionQuotas.dec(listenerName, address)
  1363	      selector.close(connectionId)
  1364	      // Call listeners to notify for closed connection.
  1365	      connectionDisconnectListeners.foreach(listener => CoreUtils.swallow(() -> listener.onDisconnect(connectionId), this, Level.ERROR))
  1366	
  1367	      inflightResponses.remove(connectionId).foreach(response => updateRequestMetrics(response))
  1368	    }
  1369	  }

  1371	  /**
  1372	   * Queue up a new connection for reading

  1374	  def accept(socketChannel: SocketChannel,
  1375	             mayBlock: Boolean,
  1376	             acceptorIdlePercentMeter: com.yammer.metrics.core.Meter): Boolean = {
  1377	    val accepted = {
  1378	      if (newConnections.offer(socketChannel))
  1379	        true
  1380	      else if (mayBlock) {
  1381	        val startNs = time.nanoseconds
  1382	        newConnections.put(socketChannel)
  1383	        acceptorIdlePercentMeter.mark(time.nanoseconds() - startNs)
  1384	        true
  1385	      } else
  1386	        false
  1387	    }
  1388	    if (accepted)
  1389	      wakeup()
  1390	    accepted
  1391	  }

  1393	  /**
  1394	   * Register any new connections that have been queued up. The number of connections processed
  1395	   * in each iteration is limited to ensure that traffic and connection close notifications of
  1396	   * existing channels are handled promptly.
  1397	   */
  1398	  private def configureNewConnections(): Unit = {
  1399	    var connectionsProcessed = 0
  1400	    while (connectionsProcessed < connectionQueueSize && !newConnections.isEmpty) {
  1401	      val channel = newConnections.poll()
  1402	      try {
  1403	        debug(s"Processor $id listening to new connection from ${channel.socket.getRemoteSocketAddress}")
  1404	        selector.register(connectionId(channel.socket), channel)
  1405	        connectionsProcessed += 1
  1406	      } catch {
  1407	        // We explicitly catch all exceptions and close the socket to avoid a socket leak.
  1408	        case e: Throwable =>
  1409	          val remoteAddress = channel.socket.getRemoteSocketAddress
  1410	          // need to close the channel here to avoid a socket leak.
  1411	          connectionQuotas.closeChannel(this, listenerName, channel)
  1412	          processException(s"Processor $id closed connection from $remoteAddress", e)
  1413	      }
  1414	    }
  1415	  }

  1420	   * channels through the usual selector.close(id) path, then close the selector itself.

  1426	    }
  1427	    while (!newConnections.isEmpty) {
  1428	      newConnections.poll().close()

  1430	    selector.channels.forEach { channel =>
  1431	      close(channel.id)
  1432	    }
  1433	    selector.close()

  1480	  def start(): Unit = {
  1481	    if (!started.getAndSet(true)) {
  1482	      // io_uring: bind the per-Processor listener here, not in the Processor constructor.
  1483	      // Acceptor.start() is the gate that runs only after SocketServer.enableRequestProcessing's
  1484	      // authorizerFuture resolves, so binding here means the LISTEN socket appears to the network
  1485	      // exactly when the broker is ready to handle requests — mirroring the NIO deferred-open
  1486	      // contract at line 552 (the NIO Acceptor opens its ServerSocketChannel here, not in its
  1487	      // constructor, "so that systems which assume that the socket being open indicates readiness
  1488	      // are not confused"). If start() is never called (broker shutdown between construction and
  1489	      // enableRequestProcessing), the listener's close() handles the unbind-less cleanup path.
  1490	      ioBundle.listener.foreach(_.start())
  1491	      thread.start()
  1492	    }
  1493	  }
  1494

  1500	  def beginShutdown(): Unit = {
  1501	    if (shouldRun.getAndSet(false)) {
  1502	      wakeup()
  1503	    }

  1506	  def close(): Unit = {
  1507	    try {
  1508	      beginShutdown()
  1509	      thread.join()
  1510	      if (!started.get) {
  1511	        CoreUtils.swallow(closeAll(), this, Level.ERROR)
  1512	      }

  1514	      metricsGroup.removeMetric("IdlePercent", Map("networkProcessor" -> id.toString).asJava)
  1515	      metrics.removeMetric(expiredConnectionsKilledCountMetricName)
  1516	    }
  1517	  }

  1520	class ConnectionQuotas(config: KafkaConfig, time: Time, metrics: Metrics) extends Logging with AutoCloseable {
  1521	
  1522	  @volatile private var defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp: Int = config.maxConnectionsPerIp
  1523	  @volatile private var maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides = config.maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides.map { case (host, count) => (InetAddress.getByName(host), count) }
  1524	  @volatile private var brokerMaxConnections = config.maxConnections
  1525	  private val interBrokerListenerName = config.interBrokerListenerName
  1526	  private val counts = mutable.Map[InetAddress, Int]()
  1527	
  1528	  // Listener counts and configs are synchronized on `counts`
  1529	  private val listenerCounts = mutable.Map[ListenerName, Int]()
  1530	  private[network] val maxConnectionsPerListener = mutable.Map[ListenerName, ListenerConnectionQuota]()
  1531	  @volatile private var totalCount = 0
  1532	  // updates to defaultConnectionRatePerIp or connectionRatePerIp must be synchronized on `counts`
  1533	  @volatile private var defaultConnectionRatePerIp = QuotaConfig.IP_CONNECTION_RATE_DEFAULT.intValue()
  1534	  private val connectionRatePerIp = new ConcurrentHashMap[InetAddress, Int]()
  1535	  // sensor that tracks broker-wide connection creation rate and limit (quota)
  1536	  private val brokerConnectionRateSensor = getOrCreateConnectionRateQuotaSensor(config.maxConnectionCreationRate, ConnectionQuotaEntity.brokerQuotaEntity())
  1537	  private val maxThrottleTimeMs = TimeUnit.SECONDS.toMillis(config.quotaConfig.quotaWindowSizeSeconds.toLong)
  1538	
  1539	  def inc(listenerName: ListenerName, address: InetAddress, acceptorBlockedPercentMeter: com.yammer.metrics.core.Meter): Unit = {
  1540	    counts.synchronized {
  1541	      waitForConnectionSlot(listenerName, acceptorBlockedPercentMeter)
  1542	
  1543	      recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle(listenerName, address)
  1544	      val count = counts.getOrElseUpdate(address, 0)
  1545	      counts.put(address, count + 1)
  1546	      totalCount += 1
  1547	      if (listenerCounts.contains(listenerName)) {
  1548	        listenerCounts.put(listenerName, listenerCounts(listenerName) + 1)
  1549	      }
  1550	      val max = maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides.getOrElse(address, defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp)
  1551	      if (count >= max)
  1552	        throw new TooManyConnectionsException(address, max)
  1553	    }
  1554	  }
  1555	
  1556	  /**
  1557	   * Non-blocking variant of [[inc]] used by the io_uring Processor accept path.
  1558	   *
  1559	   * On the NIO path [[inc]] blocks the dedicated Acceptor thread when the broker is at
  1560	   * max.connections or when the listener connection-rate quota requires a throttle.
  1561	   * On the io_uring path the caller is the Processor's poll loop — blocking it would
  1562	   * stall every other channel on that Processor, including in-flight requests. This
  1563	   * method instead refuses (returns false) when the slot is unavailable or a throttle
  1564	   * applies, after un-recording the rate-sensor sample so accounting stays correct.
  1565	   *
  1566	   * Like [[inc]], may throw [[TooManyConnectionsException]] (per-IP max) or
  1567	   * [[ConnectionThrottledException]] (per-IP rate); callers MUST close the channel on
  1568	   * either return-false or exception.
  1569	   */
  1570	  def tryInc(listenerName: ListenerName, address: InetAddress): Boolean = {
  1571	    counts.synchronized {
  1572	      val timeMs = time.milliseconds
  1573	      val throttleTimeMs = math.max(recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerName, timeMs), 0)
  1574	      if (throttleTimeMs > 0 || !connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName)) {
  1575	        // Un-record the speculative +1 from recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs so the
  1576	        // rate sensor doesn't drift when we refuse the connection. Mirrors the un-record
  1577	        // path used by recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle for IP throttle refusal.
  1578	        if (!protectedListener(listenerName)) {
  1579	          brokerConnectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false)
  1580	        }
  1581	        maxConnectionsPerListener
  1582	          .get(listenerName)
  1583	          .foreach(_.connectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false))
  1584	        return false
  1585	      }
  1586	
  1587	      recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle(listenerName, address)
  1588	      val count = counts.getOrElseUpdate(address, 0)
  1589	      counts.put(address, count + 1)
  1590	      totalCount += 1
  1591	      if (listenerCounts.contains(listenerName)) {
  1592	        listenerCounts.put(listenerName, listenerCounts(listenerName) + 1)
  1593	      }
  1594	      val max = maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides.getOrElse(address, defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp)
  1595	      if (count >= max)
  1596	        throw new TooManyConnectionsException(address, max)
  1597	      true
  1598	    }
  1599	  }
  1600	
  1601	  private[network] def updateMaxConnectionsPerIp(maxConnectionsPerIp: Int): Unit = {
  1602	    defaultMaxConnectionsPerIp = maxConnectionsPerIp
  1603	  }
  1604	
  1605	  private[network] def updateMaxConnectionsPerIpOverride(overrideQuotas: Map[String, Int]): Unit = {
  1606	    maxConnectionsPerIpOverrides = overrideQuotas.map { case (host, count) => (InetAddress.getByName(host), count) }
  1607	  }
  1608	
  1609	  private[network] def updateBrokerMaxConnections(maxConnections: Int): Unit = {
  1610	    counts.synchronized {
  1611	      brokerMaxConnections = maxConnections
  1612	      counts.notifyAll()
  1613	    }
  1614	  }
  1615	
  1616	  private[network] def updateBrokerMaxConnectionRate(maxConnectionRate: Int): Unit = {
  1617	    // if there is a connection waiting on the rate throttle delay, we will let it wait the original delay even if
  1618	    // the rate limit increases, because it is just one connection per listener and the code is simpler that way
  1619	    updateConnectionRateQuota(maxConnectionRate, ConnectionQuotaEntity.brokerQuotaEntity())
  1620	  }
  1621	
  1622	  /**
  1623	   * Update the connection rate quota for a given IP and updates quota configs for updated IPs.
  1624	   * If an IP is given, metric config will be updated only for the given IP, otherwise
  1625	   * all metric configs will be checked and updated if required.
  1626	   *
  1627	   * @param ip ip to update or default if None
  1628	   * @param maxConnectionRate new connection rate, or resets entity to default if None
  1629	   */
  1630	  def updateIpConnectionRateQuota(ip: Option[InetAddress], maxConnectionRate: Option[Int]): Unit = synchronized {
  1631	    def isIpConnectionRateMetric(metricName: MetricName) = {
  1632	      metricName.name == ConnectionQuotaEntity.CONNECTION_RATE_METRIC_NAME &&
  1633	      metricName.group == MetricsGroup &&
  1634	      metricName.tags.containsKey(ConnectionQuotaEntity.IP_METRIC_TAG)
  1635	    }
  1636	
  1637	    def shouldUpdateQuota(metric: KafkaMetric, quotaLimit: Int) = {
  1638	      quotaLimit != metric.config.quota.bound
  1639	    }
  1640

  1678	  private[network] def addListener(config: KafkaConfig, listenerName: ListenerName): Unit = {
  1679	    counts.synchronized {
  1680	      if (!maxConnectionsPerListener.contains(listenerName)) {
  1681	        val newListenerQuota = new ListenerConnectionQuota(counts, listenerName)
  1682	        maxConnectionsPerListener.put(listenerName, newListenerQuota)
  1683	        listenerCounts.put(listenerName, 0)
  1684	        config.addReconfigurable(newListenerQuota)
  1685	        newListenerQuota.configure(config.valuesWithPrefixOverride(listenerName.configPrefix))
  1686	      }
  1687	      counts.notifyAll()
  1688	    }
  1689	  }
  1690	
  1691	  private[network] def removeListener(config: KafkaConfig, listenerName: ListenerName): Unit = {
  1692	    counts.synchronized {
  1693	      maxConnectionsPerListener.remove(listenerName).foreach { listenerQuota =>
  1694	        listenerCounts.remove(listenerName)
  1695	        // once listener is removed from maxConnectionsPerListener, no metrics will be recorded into listener's sensor
  1696	        // so it is safe to remove sensor here
  1697	        listenerQuota.close()
  1698	        counts.notifyAll() // wake up any waiting acceptors to close cleanly
  1699	        config.removeReconfigurable(listenerQuota)
  1700	      }
  1701	    }
  1702	  }

  1704	  def dec(listenerName: ListenerName, address: InetAddress): Unit = {
  1705	    counts.synchronized {
  1706	      val count = counts.getOrElse(address,
  1707	        throw new IllegalArgumentException(s"Attempted to decrease connection count for address with no connections, address: $address"))
  1708	      if (count == 1)
  1709	        counts.remove(address)
  1710	      else
  1711	        counts.put(address, count - 1)
  1712	
  1713	      if (totalCount <= 0)
  1714	        error(s"Attempted to decrease total connection count for broker with no connections")
  1715	      totalCount -= 1
  1716	
  1717	      if (maxConnectionsPerListener.contains(listenerName)) {
  1718	        val listenerCount = listenerCounts(listenerName)
  1719	        if (listenerCount == 0)
  1720	          error(s"Attempted to decrease connection count for listener $listenerName with no connections")
  1721	        else
  1722	          listenerCounts.put(listenerName, listenerCount - 1)
  1723	      }
  1724	      counts.notifyAll() // wake up any acceptors waiting to process a new connection since listener connection limit was reached
  1725	    }
  1726	  }
  1727	
  1728	  def get(address: InetAddress): Int = counts.synchronized {
  1729	    counts.getOrElse(address, 0)
  1730	  }
  1731	
  1732	  private def waitForConnectionSlot(listenerName: ListenerName,
  1733	                                    acceptorBlockedPercentMeter: com.yammer.metrics.core.Meter): Unit = {
  1734	    counts.synchronized {
  1735	      val startThrottleTimeMs = time.milliseconds
  1736	      val throttleTimeMs = math.max(recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerName, startThrottleTimeMs), 0)
  1737	
  1738	      if (throttleTimeMs > 0 || !connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName)) {
  1739	        val startNs = time.nanoseconds
  1740	        val endThrottleTimeMs = startThrottleTimeMs + throttleTimeMs
  1741	        var remainingThrottleTimeMs = throttleTimeMs
  1742	        do {
  1743	          counts.wait(remainingThrottleTimeMs)
  1744	          remainingThrottleTimeMs = math.max(endThrottleTimeMs - time.milliseconds, 0)
  1745	        } while (remainingThrottleTimeMs > 0 || !connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName))
  1746	        acceptorBlockedPercentMeter.mark(time.nanoseconds - startNs)
  1747	      }
  1748	    }
  1749	  }
  1750	
  1751	  // This is invoked in every poll iteration and we close one LRU connection in an iteration
  1752	  // if necessary
  1753	  def maxConnectionsExceeded(listenerName: ListenerName): Boolean = {
  1754	    totalCount > brokerMaxConnections && !protectedListener(listenerName)
  1755	  }
  1756	
  1757	  private def connectionSlotAvailable(listenerName: ListenerName): Boolean = {
  1758	    if (listenerCounts(listenerName) >= maxListenerConnections(listenerName))
  1759	      false
  1760	    else if (protectedListener(listenerName))
  1761	      true
  1762	    else
  1763	      totalCount < brokerMaxConnections
  1764	  }
  1765	
  1766	  private def protectedListener(listenerName: ListenerName): Boolean =
  1767	    interBrokerListenerName == listenerName && listenerCounts.size > 1
  1768	
  1769	  private def maxListenerConnections(listenerName: ListenerName): Int =
  1770	    maxConnectionsPerListener.get(listenerName).map(_.maxConnections).getOrElse(Int.MaxValue)
  1771	
  1772	  /**
  1773	   * Calculates the delay needed to bring the observed connection creation rate to listener-level limit or to broker-wide
  1774	   * limit, whichever the longest. The delay is capped to the quota window size defined by QuotaWindowSizeSecondsProp
  1775	   *
  1776	   * @param listenerName listener for which calculate the delay
  1777	   * @param timeMs current time in milliseconds
  1778	   * @return delay in milliseconds
  1779	   */
  1780	  private def recordConnectionAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerName: ListenerName, timeMs: Long): Long = {
  1781	    def recordAndGetListenerThrottleTime(minThrottleTimeMs: Int): Int = {
  1782	      maxConnectionsPerListener
  1783	        .get(listenerName)
  1784	        .map { listenerQuota =>
  1785	          val listenerThrottleTimeMs = recordAndGetThrottleTimeMs(listenerQuota.connectionRateSensor, timeMs)
  1786	          val throttleTimeMs = math.max(minThrottleTimeMs, listenerThrottleTimeMs)
  1787	          // record throttle time due to hitting connection rate quota
  1788	          if (throttleTimeMs > 0) {
  1789	            listenerQuota.listenerConnectionRateThrottleSensor.record(throttleTimeMs.toDouble, timeMs)
  1790	          }
  1791	          throttleTimeMs
  1792	        }
  1793	        .getOrElse(0)
  1794	    }
  1795	
  1796	    if (protectedListener(listenerName)) {
  1797	      recordAndGetListenerThrottleTime(0)
  1798	    } else {
  1799	      val brokerThrottleTimeMs = recordAndGetThrottleTimeMs(brokerConnectionRateSensor, timeMs)
  1800	      recordAndGetListenerThrottleTime(brokerThrottleTimeMs)
  1801	    }
  1802	  }
  1803	
  1804	  /**
  1805	   * Record IP throttle time on the corresponding listener. To avoid over-recording listener/broker connection rate, we
  1806	   * also un-record the listener and broker connection if the IP gets throttled.
  1807	   *
  1808	   * @param listenerName listener to un-record connection
  1809	   * @param throttleMs IP throttle time to record for listener
  1810	   * @param timeMs current time in milliseconds
  1811	   */
  1812	  private def updateListenerMetrics(listenerName: ListenerName, throttleMs: Long, timeMs: Long): Unit = {
  1813	    if (!protectedListener(listenerName)) {
  1814	      brokerConnectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false)
  1815	    }
  1816	    maxConnectionsPerListener
  1817	      .get(listenerName)
  1818	      .foreach { listenerQuota =>
  1819	        listenerQuota.ipConnectionRateThrottleSensor.record(throttleMs.toDouble, timeMs)
  1820	        listenerQuota.connectionRateSensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false)
  1821	      }
  1822	  }
  1823	
  1824	  /**
  1825	   * Calculates the delay needed to bring the observed connection creation rate to the IP limit.
  1826	   * If the connection would cause an IP quota violation, un-record the connection for both IP,
  1827	   * listener, and broker connection rate and throw a ConnectionThrottledException. Calls to
  1828	   * this function must be performed with the counts lock to ensure that reading the IP
  1829	   * connection rate quota and creating the sensor's metric config is atomic.
  1830	   *
  1831	   * @param listenerName listener to unrecord connection if throttled
  1832	   * @param address ip address to record connection
  1833	   */
  1834	  private def recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle(listenerName: ListenerName, address: InetAddress): Unit = {
  1835	    val connectionRateQuota = connectionRateForIp(address)
  1836	    val quotaEnabled = connectionRateQuota != QuotaConfig.IP_CONNECTION_RATE_DEFAULT
  1837	    if (quotaEnabled) {
  1838	      val sensor = getOrCreateConnectionRateQuotaSensor(connectionRateQuota, ConnectionQuotaEntity.ipQuotaEntity(address))
  1839	      val timeMs = time.milliseconds
  1840	      val throttleMs = recordAndGetThrottleTimeMs(sensor, timeMs)
  1841	      if (throttleMs > 0) {
  1842	        trace(s"Throttling $address for $throttleMs ms")
  1843	        // unrecord the connection since we won't accept the connection
  1844	        sensor.record(-1.0, timeMs, false)
  1845	        updateListenerMetrics(listenerName, throttleMs, timeMs)
  1846	        throw new ConnectionThrottledException(address, timeMs, throttleMs)
  1847	      }
  1848	    }
  1849	  }
  1850	
  1851	  /**
  1852	   * Records a new connection into a given connection acceptance rate sensor 'sensor' and returns throttle time
  1853	   * in milliseconds if quota got violated
  1854	   * @param sensor sensor to record connection
  1855	   * @param timeMs current time in milliseconds
  1856	   * @return throttle time in milliseconds if quota got violated, otherwise 0
  1857	   */
  1858	  private def recordAndGetThrottleTimeMs(sensor: Sensor, timeMs: Long): Int = {
  1859	    try {
  1860	      sensor.record(1.0, timeMs)
  1861	      0
  1862	    } catch {
  1863	      case e: QuotaViolationException =>
  1864	        val throttleTimeMs = QuotaUtils.boundedThrottleTime(e, maxThrottleTimeMs, timeMs).toInt
  1865	        debug(s"Quota violated for sensor (${sensor.name}). Delay time: $throttleTimeMs ms")
  1866	        throttleTimeMs
  1867	    }
  1868	  }
  1869	
  1870	  /**
  1871	   * Creates sensor for tracking the connection creation rate and corresponding connection rate quota for a given
  1872	   * listener or broker-wide, if listener is not provided.
  1873	   * @param quotaLimit connection creation rate quota
  1874	   * @param connectionQuotaEntity entity to create the sensor for
  1875	   */
  1876	  private def getOrCreateConnectionRateQuotaSensor(quotaLimit: Int, connectionQuotaEntity: ConnectionQuotaEntity): Sensor = {
  1877	    Option(metrics.getSensor(connectionQuotaEntity.sensorName)).getOrElse {
  1878	      val sensor = metrics.sensor(
  1879	        connectionQuotaEntity.sensorName,
  1880	        rateQuotaMetricConfig(quotaLimit),
  1881	        connectionQuotaEntity.sensorExpiration
  1882	      )
  1883	      sensor.add(connectionRateMetricName(connectionQuotaEntity), new Rate, null)
  1884	      sensor
  1885	    }
  1886	  }
  1887	
  1888	  /**
  1889	   * Updates quota configuration for a given connection quota entity
  1890	   */

   822	/**
   823	 * The pair of I/O objects a Processor owns. For NIO listeners {@code listener} is None and
   824	 * the Acceptor binds the server socket. For io_uring listeners {@code listener} is Some and
   825	 * holds the per-Processor IoUringServerListener (bound on the same address via
   826	 * SO_REUSEPORT). Closing the bundle tears the listener down before the selector so accepted
   827	 * channels still flow through {@code onDisconnect} before the selector is gone.
   828	 */
   829	private[network] case class ProcessorIoBundle(
   830	  selector: BrokerSelector,
   831	  listener: Option[org.apache.kafka.network.iouring.IoUringServerListener]) {
   832	  def close(): Unit = {
   833	    listener.foreach(l => try l.close() catch { case _: Throwable => /* logged downstream */ })
   834	    selector.close()
   835	  }
   836	}
   837	
   838	private[kafka] object Processor {
   839	  private val IdlePercentMetricName = "IdlePercent"
   840	  val NetworkProcessorMetricTag = "networkProcessor"
   841	  val ListenerMetricTag = "listener"
   842	  val ConnectionQueueSize = 20
   843	
   844	  private[network] def parseRequestHeader(apiVersionManager: ApiVersionManager, buffer: ByteBuffer): RequestHeader = {
   845	    val header = RequestHeader.parse(buffer)
   846	    if (apiVersionManager.isApiEnabled(header.apiKey, header.apiVersion)) {
   847	      header
   848	    } else if (header.isApiVersionSupported()) {
   849	      throw new InvalidRequestException(s"Received request for disabled api with key ${header.apiKey.id} (${header.apiKey().name}) and version ${header.apiVersion}")
   850	    } else {
   851	      throw new UnsupportedVersionException(s"Received request for api with key ${header.apiKey.id} (${header.apiKey().name}) and unsupported version ${header.apiVersion}")
   852	    }
   853	  }
   854	}
   855	
   856	/**
   857	 * Thread that processes all requests from a single connection. There are N of these running in parallel
   858	 * each of which has its own selector
   859	 *
   860	 * @param isPrivilegedListener The privileged listener flag is used as one factor to determine whether
   861	 *                             a certain request is forwarded or not. When the control plane is defined,
   862	 *                             the control plane processor would be fellow broker's choice for sending
   863	 *                             forwarding requests; if the control plane is not defined, the processor
   864	 *                             relying on the inter broker listener would be acting as the privileged listener.
   865	 */
   866	private[kafka] class Processor(
   867	  val id: Int,
   868	  time: Time,
   869	  maxRequestSize: Int,
   870	  requestChannel: RequestChannel,
   871	  connectionQuotas: ConnectionQuotas,
   872	  connectionsMaxIdleMs: Long,
   873	  failedAuthenticationDelayMs: Int,
   874	  listenerName: ListenerName,
   875	  securityProtocol: SecurityProtocol,
   876	  // Bind address for this listener — used by the io_uring path so each Processor can stand
   877	  // up its own IoUringServerListener on the configured port via SO_REUSEPORT. Unused on the
   878	  // NIO path (where the Acceptor binds once and hands SocketChannels to register()).
   879	  endPoint: EndPoint,
   880	  config: KafkaConfig,
   881	  metrics: Metrics,
   882	  credentialProvider: CredentialProvider,
   883	  memoryPool: MemoryPool,
   884	  logContext: LogContext,
   885	  connectionQueueSize: Int,
   886	  isPrivilegedListener: Boolean,
   887	  apiVersionManager: ApiVersionManager,
   888	  threadName: String,
   889	  connectionDisconnectListeners: Seq[ConnectionDisconnectListener],
   890	  // Acceptor-owned Yammer meter that gates connectionQuotas.inc. The Acceptor uses it on the
   891	  // NIO accept path; the Processor needs it on the io_uring path because that's where inc()
   892	  // runs (Netty's event loop is upstream of us, not Acceptor.accept).
   893	  acceptorBlockedPercentMeter: com.yammer.metrics.core.Meter
   894	) extends Runnable with Logging {
   895	  private val metricsGroup = new KafkaMetricsGroup(this.getClass)
   896	
   897	  val shouldRun: AtomicBoolean = new AtomicBoolean(true)
   898	  private val started: AtomicBoolean = new AtomicBoolean()
   899	
   900	  val thread: KafkaThread = KafkaThread.nonDaemon(threadName, this)
   901	
   902	  private val newConnections = new ArrayBlockingQueue[SocketChannel](connectionQueueSize)
   903	  private val inflightResponses = mutable.Map[String, RequestChannel.Response]()
   904	  private val responseQueue = new LinkedBlockingDeque[RequestChannel.Response]()
   905	
   906	  private[kafka] val metricTags = mutable.LinkedHashMap(
   907	    ListenerMetricTag -> listenerName.value,
   908	    NetworkProcessorMetricTag -> id.toString
   909	  ).asJava
   910	
   911	  metricsGroup.newGauge(IdlePercentMetricName, () => {
   912	    Option(metrics.metric(metrics.metricName("io-wait-ratio", MetricsGroup, metricTags))).fold(0.0)(m =>
   913	      Math.min(m.metricValue.asInstanceOf[Double], 1.0))
   914	  },
   915	    // for compatibility, only add a networkProcessor tag to the Yammer Metrics alias (the equivalent Selector metric
   916	    // also includes the listener name)
   917	    Map(NetworkProcessorMetricTag -> id.toString).asJava
   918	  )
   919	
   920	  private val expiredConnectionsKilledCount = new CumulativeSum()
   921	  private val expiredConnectionsKilledCountMetricName = metrics.metricName("expired-connections-killed-count", MetricsGroup, metricTags)
   922	  metrics.addMetric(expiredConnectionsKilledCountMetricName, expiredConnectionsKilledCount)
   923	
   924	  // The selector is a BrokerSelector so an alternative I/O backend (e.g. Netty io_uring)
   925	  // can be swapped in without modifying clients/. The default path keeps using the Java-NIO
   926	  // KSelector — we just wrap it in a thin NioBrokerSelector adapter so Processor talks to a
   927	  // single broker-side type.
   928	  //
   929	  // For listeners resolved to io_uring (Linux + PLAINTEXT + config knob), we construct an
   930	  // IoUringSelector + IoUringServerListener pair instead. The listener binds the same address
   931	  // as the Acceptor's endPoint via SO_REUSEPORT (the kernel shards accepts across all
   932	  // io_uring Processors on this listener), and the Acceptor's own NIO bind+accept is skipped
   933	  // — see Acceptor.start()/run() guards.
   934	  private[network] val ioBundle: ProcessorIoBundle = buildIoBundle()
   935	  private[network] val selector: BrokerSelector = ioBundle.selector
   936	
   937	  private def buildIoBundle(): ProcessorIoBundle = {
   938	    if (config.usesIoUring(securityProtocol)) {
   939	      if (endPoint.port == 0) {
   940	        throw new KafkaException(
   941	          s"io_uring listener ${endPoint.listenerName} requires an explicit port " +
   942	          "(SO_REUSEPORT sharding needs every Processor to bind on the same configured port; " +
   943	          "wildcard port 0 is not supported in this version)")
   944	      }
   945	      // Threaded to IoUringPlaintextAuthenticator so a user-configured PRINCIPAL_BUILDER_CLASS_CONFIG
   946	      // is honored on this listener — mirroring the NIO PlaintextChannelBuilder path which calls
   947	      // configure(channelBuilderConfigs(config, listenerName)).
   948	      val ioUringChannelConfigs = ioUringChannelBuilderConfigs()
   949	      val ioUringSelector = new org.apache.kafka.network.iouring.IoUringSelector(
   950	        listenerName,
   951	        maxRequestSize,
   952	        memoryPool,
   953	        java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS.toNanos(connectionsMaxIdleMs),
   954	        time,
   955	        id,
   956	        ioUringChannelConfigs)
   957	      // Mirror Acceptor.openServerSocket (SocketServer.scala:657-662): a wildcard listener
   958	      // configured as PLAINTEXT://:9092 has endPoint.host == null/blank, and
   959	      // InetSocketAddress(null, port) throws IllegalArgumentException. We must use the
   960	      // wildcard form for blank hosts so the io_uring path binds 0.0.0.0:port, matching
   961	      // NIO. Without this guard the default config (auto + Linux + PLAINTEXT://:9092)
   962	      // crashes broker startup before any connection is accepted.
   963	      val bindAddress =
   964	        if (Utils.isBlank(endPoint.host)) new java.net.InetSocketAddress(endPoint.port)
   965	        else new java.net.InetSocketAddress(endPoint.host, endPoint.port)
   966	      // Apply socket.send.buffer.bytes/socket.receive.buffer.bytes so io_uring matches the
   967	      // NIO Acceptor parity at SocketServer.scala:740-746 / openServerSocket(..., recvBuf).
   968	      // Without this, an operator-tuned SO_SNDBUF/SO_RCVBUF silently has no effect on the
   969	      // io_uring listener and per-connection throughput diverges from the NIO baseline on
   970	      // the same broker.
   971	      val listener = new org.apache.kafka.network.iouring.IoUringServerListener(
   972	        bindAddress,
   973	        ioUringSelector,
   974	        config.socketListenBacklogSize,
   975	        config.socketSendBufferBytes,
   976	        config.socketReceiveBufferBytes)
   977	      ProcessorIoBundle(ioUringSelector, Some(listener))
   978	    } else {
   979	      ProcessorIoBundle(new NioBrokerSelector(createSelector(
   980	        ChannelBuilders.serverChannelBuilder(
   981	          listenerName,
   982	          listenerName == config.interBrokerListenerName,
   983	          securityProtocol,
   984	          config,
   985	          credentialProvider.credentialCache,
   986	          credentialProvider.tokenCache,
   987	          time,
   988	          logContext,
   989	          version => apiVersionManager.apiVersionResponse(throttleTimeMs = 0, version < 4)
   990	        )
   991	      )), None)
   992	    }
   993	  }
   994	
   995	  /**
   996	   * Build the configs map handed to IoUringPlaintextAuthenticator's principal builder,
   997	   * mirroring ChannelBuilders.channelBuilderConfigs (package-private under clients/).
   998	   *
   999	   * The NIO PlaintextChannelBuilder receives a map that merges
  1000	   * config.valuesWithPrefixOverride(listenerPrefix) (the parsed view, with per-listener
  1001	   * overrides applied) with config.originals() (the raw user-provided keys, including any
  1002	   * keys NOT in the schema such as principal.builder.* custom keys). A Configurable
  1003	   * principal builder reads this combined map in configure().
  1004	   *
  1005	   * If the io_uring path only forwarded valuesWithPrefixOverride, a custom builder whose
  1006	   * configure() reads non-schema keys would see them on a NIO listener but not on an
  1007	   * io_uring listener — a silent transport-level divergence. Replicate the merge here so
  1008	   * the two transports agree on what the user said.
  1009	   */
  1010	  private def ioUringChannelBuilderConfigs(): java.util.Map[String, _] = {
  1011	    val parsedConfigs = new java.util.HashMap[String, AnyRef]()
  1012	    parsedConfigs.putAll(config.valuesWithPrefixOverride(listenerName.configPrefix))
  1013	    val listenerPrefix = listenerName.configPrefix
  1014	    config.originals.entrySet.forEach { e =>
  1015	      val key = e.getKey
  1016	      // Skip: already in parsed map.
  1017	      if (parsedConfigs.containsKey(key)) {
  1018	        // no-op
  1019	      // Skip: listener-prefixed duplicate where the unprefixed key is already parsed.
  1020	      } else if (key.startsWith(listenerPrefix) &&
  1021	                 parsedConfigs.containsKey(key.substring(listenerPrefix.length))) {
  1022	        // no-op
  1023	      } else {
  1024	        // Mirrors ChannelBuilders.channelBuilderConfigs' third filter: drop keys whose
  1025	        // first-dot-stripped suffix is already in parsed (e.g. mechanism-prefixed keys
  1026	        // like "PLAIN.some.prop" when "some.prop" exists).
  1027	        val dotIdx = key.indexOf('.')
  1028	        if (dotIdx >= 0 && parsedConfigs.containsKey(key.substring(dotIdx + 1))) {
  1029	          // no-op
  1030	        } else {
  1031	          parsedConfigs.put(key, e.getValue)
  1032	        }
  1033	      }
  1034	    }
  1035	    parsedConfigs
  1036	  }
  1037	
  1038	  // Visible to override for testing. Returns the underlying Java-NIO KSelector so existing
  1039	  // TestableSelector overrides keep working unchanged — the Processor wraps it above.
  1040	  protected[network] def createSelector(channelBuilder: ChannelBuilder): KSelector = {
  1041	    channelBuilder match {
  1042	      case reconfigurable: Reconfigurable => config.addReconfigurable(reconfigurable)
  1043	      case _ =>
  1044	    }
  1045	    new KSelector(
  1046	      maxRequestSize,
  1047	      connectionsMaxIdleMs,
  1048	      failedAuthenticationDelayMs,
  1049	      metrics,
  1050	      time,
  1051	      "socket-server",
  1052	      metricTags,
  1053	      false,
  1054	      true,
  1055	      channelBuilder,
  1056	      memoryPool,
  1057	      logContext)
  1058	  }
  1059	
  1060	  // Connection ids have the format `localAddr:localPort-remoteAddr:remotePort-index`. The index is a
  1061	  // non-negative incrementing value that ensures that even if remotePort is reused after a connection is
  1062	  // closed, connection ids are not reused while requests from the closed connection are being processed.
  1063	  private var nextConnectionIndex = 0
  1064	
  1065	  override def run(): Unit = {
  1066	    try {
  1067	      while (shouldRun.get()) {
  1068	        try {
  1069	          // setup any new connections that have been queued up
  1070	          configureNewConnections()
  1071	          // register any new responses for writing
  1072	          processNewResponses()
  1073	          poll()
  1074	          // io_uring listeners accept on the Netty event loop, so ConnectionQuotas.inc
  1075	          // happens here (after poll) rather than in Acceptor.accept. NIO listeners have
  1076	          // already had inc applied by the Acceptor, and broker-side selector.connected()
  1077	          // is empty for them — so this is effectively a no-op on the NIO path.
  1078	          applyConnectionQuotasForNewlyAcceptedChannels()
  1079	          processCompletedReceives()
  1080	          processCompletedSends()
  1081	          processDisconnected()
  1082	          closeExcessConnections()
  1083	        } catch {
  1084	          // We catch all the throwables here to prevent the processor thread from exiting. We do this because
  1085	          // letting a processor exit might cause a bigger impact on the broker. This behavior might need to be
  1086	          // reviewed if we see an exception that needs the entire broker to stop. Usually the exceptions thrown would
  1087	          // be either associated with a specific socket channel or a bad request. These exceptions are caught and
  1088	          // processed by the individual methods above which close the failing channel and continue processing other
  1089	          // channels. So this catch block should only ever see ControlThrowables.
  1090	          case e: Throwable => processException("Processor got uncaught exception.", e)
  1091	        }
  1092	      }
  1093	    } finally {
  1094	      debug(s"Closing selector - processor $id")
  1095	      CoreUtils.swallow(closeAll(), this, Level.ERROR)
  1096	    }
  1097	  }
  1098	
  1099	  private[network] def processException(errorMessage: String, throwable: Throwable): Unit = {
  1100	    throwable match {
  1101	      case e: ControlThrowable => throw e
  1102	      case e => error(errorMessage, e)
  1103	    }
  1104	  }
  1105	
  1106	  private def processChannelException(channelId: String, errorMessage: String, throwable: Throwable): Unit = {
  1107	    if (openOrClosingChannel(channelId).isDefined) {
  1108	      error(s"Closing socket for $channelId because of error", throwable)
  1109	      close(channelId)
  1110	    }
  1111	    processException(errorMessage, throwable)
  1112	  }
  1113	
  1114	  private def processNewResponses(): Unit = {
  1115	    var currentResponse: RequestChannel.Response = null
  1116	    while ({currentResponse = dequeueResponse(); currentResponse != null}) {
  1117	      val channelId = currentResponse.request.context.connectionId
  1118	      try {
  1119	        currentResponse match {
  1120	          case response: NoOpResponse =>
  1121	            // There is no response to send to the client, we need to read more pipelined requests
  1122	            // that are sitting in the server's socket buffer
  1123	            updateRequestMetrics(response)
  1124	            trace(s"Socket server received empty response to send, registering for read: $response")
  1125	            // Try unmuting the channel. If there was no quota violation and the channel has not been throttled,
  1126	            // it will be unmuted immediately. If the channel has been throttled, it will be unmuted only if the
  1127	            // throttling delay has already passed by now.
  1128	            handleChannelMuteEvent(channelId, ChannelMuteEvent.RESPONSE_SENT)
  1129	            tryUnmuteChannel(channelId)
  1130	
  1131	          case response: SendResponse =>
  1132	            sendResponse(response, response.responseSend)
  1133	          case response: CloseConnectionResponse =>
  1134	            updateRequestMetrics(response)
  1135	            trace("Closing socket connection actively according to the response code.")
  1136	            close(channelId)
  1137	          case _: StartThrottlingResponse =>
  1138	            handleChannelMuteEvent(channelId, ChannelMuteEvent.THROTTLE_STARTED)
  1139	          case _: EndThrottlingResponse =>
  1140	            // Try unmuting the channel. The channel will be unmuted only if the response has already been sent out to
  1141	            // the client.
  1142	            handleChannelMuteEvent(channelId, ChannelMuteEvent.THROTTLE_ENDED)
  1143	            tryUnmuteChannel(channelId)
  1144	          case _ =>
  1145	            throw new IllegalArgumentException(s"Unknown response type: ${currentResponse.getClass}")
  1146	        }
  1147	      } catch {
  1148	        case e: Throwable =>
  1149	          processChannelException(channelId, s"Exception while processing response for $channelId", e)
  1150	      }
  1151	    }
  1152	  }
  1153	
  1154	  // `protected` for test usage
  1155	  protected[network] def sendResponse(response: RequestChannel.Response, responseSend: Send): Unit = {
  1156	    val connectionId = response.request.context.connectionId
  1157	    trace(s"Socket server received response to send to $connectionId, registering for write and sending data: $response")
  1158	    // `channel` can be None if the connection was closed remotely or if selector closed it for being idle for too long
  1159	    if (channel(connectionId).isEmpty) {
  1160	      warn(s"Attempting to send response via channel for which there is no open connection, connection id $connectionId")
  1161	      response.request.updateRequestMetrics(0L, response)
  1162	    }
  1163	    // Invoke send for closingChannel as well so that the send is failed and the channel closed properly and
  1164	    // removed from the Selector after discarding any pending staged receives.
  1165	    // `openOrClosingChannel` can be None if the selector closed the connection because it was idle for too long
  1166	    if (openOrClosingChannel(connectionId).isDefined) {
  1167	      selector.send(new NetworkSend(connectionId, responseSend))
  1168	      inflightResponses += (connectionId -> response)
  1169	    }
  1170	  }
  1171	
  1172	  private def poll(): Unit = {
  1173	    val pollTimeout = if (newConnections.isEmpty) 300 else 0
  1174	    try selector.poll(pollTimeout)
  1175	    catch {
  1176	      case e @ (_: IllegalStateException | _: IOException) =>
  1177	        // The exception is not re-thrown and any completed sends/receives/connections/disconnections
  1178	        // from this poll will be processed.
  1179	        error(s"Processor $id poll failed", e)
  1180	    }
  1181	  }
  1182	
  1183	  private def processCompletedReceives(): Unit = {
  1184	    selector.completedReceives.forEach { receive =>
  1185	      try {
  1186	        openOrClosingChannel(receive.source) match {
  1187	          case Some(channel) =>
  1188	            val header = parseRequestHeader(apiVersionManager, receive.payload)
  1189	            if (header.apiKey == ApiKeys.SASL_HANDSHAKE && channel.maybeBeginServerReauthentication(receive,
  1190	              () => time.nanoseconds()))
  1191	              trace(s"Begin re-authentication: $channel")
  1192	            else {
  1193	              val nowNanos = time.nanoseconds()
  1194	              if (channel.serverAuthenticationSessionExpired(nowNanos)) {
  1195	                // be sure to decrease connection count and drop any in-flight responses
  1196	                debug(s"Disconnecting expired channel: $channel : $header")
  1197	                close(channel.id)
  1198	                expiredConnectionsKilledCount.record(null, 1, 0)
  1199	              } else {
  1200	                val connectionId = receive.source
  1201	                val context = new RequestContext(header, connectionId, channel.socketAddress, Optional.of(channel.socketPort()),
  1202	                  channel.principal, listenerName, securityProtocol, channel.channelMetadataRegistry.clientInformation,
  1203	                  isPrivilegedListener, channel.principalSerde)
  1204	
  1205	                val req = new RequestChannel.Request(processor = id, context = context,
  1206	                  startTimeNanos = nowNanos, memoryPool, receive.payload, requestChannel.metrics, None)
  1207	
  1208	                // KIP-511: ApiVersionsRequest is intercepted here to catch the client software name
  1209	                // and version. It is done here to avoid wiring things up to the api layer.
  1210	                if (header.apiKey == ApiKeys.API_VERSIONS) {
  1211	                  val apiVersionsRequest = req.body[ApiVersionsRequest]
  1212	                  if (apiVersionsRequest.isValid) {
  1213	                    channel.channelMetadataRegistry.registerClientInformation(new ClientInformation(
  1214	                      apiVersionsRequest.data.clientSoftwareName,
  1215	                      apiVersionsRequest.data.clientSoftwareVersion))
  1216	                  }
  1217	                }
  1218	                requestChannel.sendRequest(req)
  1219	                selector.mute(connectionId)
  1220	                handleChannelMuteEvent(connectionId, ChannelMuteEvent.REQUEST_RECEIVED)
  1221	              }
  1222	            }
  1223	          case None =>
  1224	            // This should never happen since completed receives are processed immediately after `poll()`
  1225	            throw new IllegalStateException(s"Channel ${receive.source} removed from selector before processing completed receive")
  1226	        }
  1227	      } catch {
  1228	        // note that even though we got an exception, we can assume that receive.source is valid.
  1229	        // Issues with constructing a valid receive object were handled earlier
  1230	        case e: Throwable =>
  1231	          processChannelException(receive.source, s"Exception while processing request from ${receive.source}", e)
  1232	      }
  1233	    }
  1234	    selector.clearCompletedReceives()
  1235	  }
  1236	
  1237	  private def processCompletedSends(): Unit = {
  1238	    selector.completedSends.forEach { send =>
  1239	      try {
  1240	        val response = inflightResponses.remove(send.destinationId).getOrElse {
  1241	          throw new IllegalStateException(s"Send for ${send.destinationId} completed, but not in `inflightResponses`")
  1242	        }
  1243	
  1244	        // Invoke send completion callback, and then update request metrics since there might be some
  1245	        // request metrics got updated during callback
  1246	        response.onComplete.foreach(onComplete => onComplete(send))
  1247	        updateRequestMetrics(response)
  1248	
  1249	        // Try unmuting the channel. If there was no quota violation and the channel has not been throttled,
  1250	        // it will be unmuted immediately. If the channel has been throttled, it will unmuted only if the throttling
  1251	        // delay has already passed by now.
  1252	        handleChannelMuteEvent(send.destinationId, ChannelMuteEvent.RESPONSE_SENT)
  1253	        tryUnmuteChannel(send.destinationId)
  1254	      } catch {
  1255	        case e: Throwable => processChannelException(send.destinationId,
  1256	          s"Exception while processing completed send to ${send.destinationId}", e)
  1257	      }
  1258	    }
  1259	    selector.clearCompletedSends()
  1260	  }
  1261	
  1262	  private def updateRequestMetrics(response: RequestChannel.Response): Unit = {
  1263	    val request = response.request
  1264	    val networkThreadTimeNanos = openOrClosingChannel(request.context.connectionId).fold(0L)(_.getAndResetNetworkThreadTimeNanos())
  1265	    request.updateRequestMetrics(networkThreadTimeNanos, response)

  1267	
  1268	  /**
  1269	   * Apply ConnectionQuotas.inc for channels that just surfaced from selector.connected().
  1270	   * io_uring listeners accept inside Netty's event loop and never went through
  1271	   * Acceptor.accept (which is where NIO inc'd the quota), so the gate has to land here
  1272	   * instead.
  1273	   *
  1274	   * Refusal paths:
  1275	   *  - tryInc returns false (broker max-connections hit, or listener connection-rate
  1276	   *    throttle): no inc happened, so we just close the selector channel.
  1277	   *  - TooManyConnectionsException: tryInc increments the per-IP / listener / total
  1278	   *    counters BEFORE checking the per-IP max (see ConnectionQuotas.tryInc), so by
  1279	   *    the time the exception fires the inc has already landed. selector.close on the
  1280	   *    io_uring path is silent (it does NOT surface in selector.disconnected, so
  1281	   *    processDisconnected will not run the normal dec). Roll the inc back here.
  1282	   *  - ConnectionThrottledException: thrown by recordIpConnectionMaybeThrottle, which
  1283	   *    runs before the inc, so no rollback is needed.
  1284	   *
  1285	   * For NIO listeners selector.connected() is always empty (broker-side Processors don't
  1286	   * initiate outgoing connections), so this method is a hot-path no-op there.
  1287	   */
  1288	  private def applyConnectionQuotasForNewlyAcceptedChannels(): Unit = {
  1289	    val newlyConnected = selector.connected()
  1290	    if (newlyConnected.isEmpty) return
  1291	    newlyConnected.forEach { connectionId =>
  1292	      val channel = selector.channel(connectionId)
  1293	      if (channel != null) {
  1294	        val address = channel.socketAddress
  1295	        if (address != null) {
  1296	          try {
  1297	            // tryInc — never blocks. On NIO the dedicated Acceptor thread can wait for a
  1298	            // slot via inc(); here the caller is the Processor's poll loop, so a wait
  1299	            // would stall every other channel on this Processor.
  1300	            if (!connectionQuotas.tryInc(listenerName, address)) {
  1301	              info(s"Closing io_uring connection $connectionId from $address: broker- or " +
  1302	                s"listener-level connection slot unavailable or rate-limited.")
  1303	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1304	            }
  1305	          } catch {
  1306	            case e: TooManyConnectionsException =>
  1307	              info(s"Closing io_uring connection $connectionId from ${e.ip}: " +
  1308	                s"already has the configured maximum of ${e.count} connections.")
  1309	              // tryInc incremented counts before throwing; selector.close is silent on
  1310	              // the io_uring path, so processDisconnected will not run dec. Roll back
  1311	              // the inc explicitly, otherwise the per-IP / listener / total counters
  1312	              // leak permanently and the next refusal compares against a stale count.
  1313	              connectionQuotas.dec(listenerName, address)
  1314	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1315	            case e: ConnectionThrottledException =>
  1316	              debug(s"Closing throttled io_uring connection $connectionId from $address " +
  1317	                s"(throttle ${e.throttleTimeMs}ms)")
  1318	              selector.close(connectionId)
  1319	          }
  1320	        }
  1321	      }
  1322	    }
  1323	  }

  1325	  private def processDisconnected(): Unit = {
