    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
    18	
    19	import org.apache.kafka.common.network.KafkaChannel;
    20	import org.apache.kafka.common.network.Selectable;
    21	
    22	import java.io.IOException;
    23	import java.nio.channels.SocketChannel;
    24	import java.util.List;
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * Broker-side facade over {@link Selectable}. Captures the additional methods that the
    28	 * {@code kafka.network.Processor} relies on beyond the public {@link Selectable} interface,
    29	 * so an alternative I/O backend (Netty io_uring) can be plugged in without modifying
    30	 * {@code clients/}.
    31	 *
    32	 * <p>The contract is identical to {@code org.apache.kafka.common.network.Selector} for the
    33	 * methods listed below — see that class for invariants. Implementations are not required to
    34	 * be thread-safe; each instance is owned by a single Processor thread.
    35	 */
    36	public interface BrokerSelector extends Selectable, AutoCloseable {
    37	
    38	    /**
    39	     * Register an already-accepted server-side socket channel with the selector. The selector
    40	     * takes ownership of the channel; subsequent I/O is driven through {@link #poll(long)}.
    41	     *
    42	     * @throws IllegalStateException if a channel with this id is already registered or closing
    43	     */
    44	    void register(String id, SocketChannel socketChannel) throws IOException;
    45	
    46	    /** All channels currently registered with the selector (including closing channels in some backends). */
    47	    List<KafkaChannel> channels();
    48	
    49	    /** Look up an active channel by id, or {@code null} if none. */
    50	    KafkaChannel channel(String id);
    51	
    52	    /** Look up a channel that is in the process of being closed by id, or {@code null} if none. */
    53	    KafkaChannel closingChannel(String id);
    54	
    55	    /**
    56	     * The channel with the lowest priority (typically least-recently-used), used by the broker
    57	     * to make room when global connection quotas are exceeded.
    58	     *
    59	     * @return the lowest-priority channel, or {@code null} if no eligible channel exists
    60	     */
    61	    KafkaChannel lowestPriorityChannel();
    62	
    63	    /**
    64	     * Discard the receive-completion events produced by the most recent {@link #poll(long)}.
    65	     * Called by the broker once it has handed those receives off to request processing.
    66	     */
    67	    void clearCompletedReceives();
    68	
    69	    /**
    70	     * Discard the send-completion events produced by the most recent {@link #poll(long)}.
    71	     * Called by the broker once it has handed those sends off to response processing.
    72	     */
    73	    void clearCompletedSends();
    74	
    75	    @Override
    76	    void close();
    77	}

    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
    18	
    19	import org.apache.kafka.common.network.ChannelState;
    20	import org.apache.kafka.common.network.KafkaChannel;
    21	import org.apache.kafka.common.network.NetworkReceive;
    22	import org.apache.kafka.common.network.NetworkSend;
    23	import org.apache.kafka.common.network.Selector;
    24	
    25	import java.io.IOException;
    26	import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
    27	import java.nio.channels.SocketChannel;
    28	import java.util.Collection;
    29	import java.util.List;
    30	import java.util.Map;
    31	import java.util.Objects;
    32	
    33	/**
    34	 * Pass-through {@link BrokerSelector} backed by the production Java-NIO {@link Selector}.
    35	 *
    36	 * <p>Exists so the broker's {@code Processor} can talk to a single
    37	 * {@link BrokerSelector} type regardless of which I/O backend is active. Holds no state
    38	 * beyond a reference to the wrapped Selector — every call delegates.
    39	 */
    40	public final class NioBrokerSelector implements BrokerSelector {
    41	
    42	    private final Selector delegate;
    43	
    44	    public NioBrokerSelector(Selector delegate) {
    45	        this.delegate = Objects.requireNonNull(delegate, "delegate");
    46	    }
    47	
    48	    /** Visible to broker-side code that still needs the concrete Selector (e.g. test hooks). */
    49	    public Selector unwrap() {
    50	        return delegate;
    51	    }
    52	
    53	    @Override
    54	    public void register(String id, SocketChannel socketChannel) throws IOException {
    55	        Objects.requireNonNull(socketChannel, "socketChannel");

     1	/*
     2	 * Licensed to the Apache Software Foundation (ASF) under one or more
     3	 * contributor license agreements. See the NOTICE file distributed with

     5	 * The ASF licenses this file to You under the Apache License, Version 2.0

    12	 * distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
    13	 * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
    14	 * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
    15	 * limitations under the License.
    16	 */
    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
    18	
    19	import org.slf4j.Logger;
    20	import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
    21	
    22	import java.lang.reflect.Method;
    23	import java.util.Locale;
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * Runtime probe for whether the Netty io_uring transport is usable on this JVM/host.
    27	 *
    28	 * <p>The probe is reflective so this class itself loads on any platform — including
    29	 * macOS / Windows / Linux JVMs that don't ship the io_uring native library — without
    30	 * triggering a NoClassDefFoundError.
    31	 *
    32	 * <p>The result is cached; the underlying conditions (OS, kernel, native lib) don't
    33	 * change during a JVM run.
    34	 */
    35	public final class IoUringSupport {
    36	
    37	    private static final Logger LOG = LoggerFactory.getLogger(IoUringSupport.class);
    38	    private static final String IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS = "io.netty.channel.uring.IoUring";
    39	
    40	    private static final boolean LINUX =
    41	        System.getProperty("os.name", "").toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).contains("linux");
    42	
    43	    private static final Probe PROBE = computeProbe();
    44	
    45	    private IoUringSupport() { }
    46	
    47	    /** @return true iff this JVM is running on Linux. */
    48	    public static boolean isLinux() {
    49	        return LINUX;
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    /**
    53	     * @return true iff the Netty io_uring transport reports itself usable on this host
    54	     *         (Linux kernel with io_uring support and the native lib loadable).
    55	     */
    56	    public static boolean isAvailable() {
    57	        return PROBE.available;
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    /**
    61	     * @return a human-readable reason why io_uring is unavailable, or {@code null} when
    62	     *         {@link #isAvailable()} is {@code true}. The reason is suitable for inclusion
    63	     *         in startup logs and error messages.
    64	     */
    65	    public static String unavailabilityReason() {
    66	        return PROBE.reason;
    67	    }
    68	
    69	    private static Probe computeProbe() {
    70	        if (!LINUX) {
    71	            return new Probe(false, "io_uring requires Linux (this JVM reports os.name="
    72	                + System.getProperty("os.name") + ")");
    73	        }
    74	        try {
    75	            Class<?> ioUring = Class.forName(IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS, true, IoUringSupport.class.getClassLoader());
    76	            Method isAvailable = ioUring.getMethod("isAvailable");
    77	            boolean available = (Boolean) isAvailable.invoke(null);
    78	            if (available) {
    79	                return new Probe(true, null);
    80	            }
    81	            String cause = describeUnavailabilityCause(ioUring);
    82	            return new Probe(false, "Netty io_uring transport reports unavailable: " + cause);
    83	        } catch (ClassNotFoundException e) {
    84	            return new Probe(false, "Netty io_uring transport classes not on the classpath ("
    85	                + IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS + ")");
    86	        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
    87	            LOG.debug("Failed to probe Netty io_uring availability via reflection", e);
    88	            return new Probe(false, "Reflective probe of " + IO_URING_PROBE_CLASS
    89	                + " failed: " + e.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
    90	        }
    91	    }
    92	
    93	    private static String describeUnavailabilityCause(Class<?> ioUring) {
    94	        try {
    95	            Method unavailabilityCause = ioUring.getMethod("unavailabilityCause");
    96	            Object cause = unavailabilityCause.invoke(null);
    97	            if (cause == null) return "no cause reported";
    98	            return cause.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": " + ((Throwable) cause).getMessage();
    99	        } catch (ReflectiveOperationException e) {
   100	            return "unavailabilityCause() unreadable (" + e.getClass().getSimpleName() + ")";
   101	        }
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    private static final class Probe {
   105	        final boolean available;
   106	        final String reason;
   107	        Probe(boolean available, String reason) {
   108	            this.available = available;
   109	            this.reason = reason;
   110	        }
   111	    }
   112	}

     1	/*

    13	 * WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
    14	 * See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
    15	 * limitations under the License.
    16	 */
    17	package org.apache.kafka.network.iouring;
    18	
    19	import org.apache.kafka.common.config.ConfigException;
    20	import org.apache.kafka.network.SocketServerConfigs;
    21	
    22	import java.util.Arrays;
    23	import java.util.Locale;
    24	import java.util.stream.Collectors;
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * Which broker-side I/O backend to use for accepting and serving client connections.
    28	 *
    29	 * <p>Selection happens once at broker startup; there is no per-request dispatch.
    30	 * {@link #AUTO} resolves to {@link #IO_URING} on a Linux host with kernel io_uring support and
    31	 * a PLAINTEXT listener, and to {@link #NIO} everywhere else.
    32	 */
    33	public enum SelectorImplementation {
    34	    /** Java NIO Selector-based path; the historical Kafka default. */
    35	    NIO("nio"),
    36	    /** Netty io_uring-backed path; Linux + PLAINTEXT only in this version. */
    37	    IO_URING("io_uring"),
    38	    /** Pick {@link #IO_URING} when supported, otherwise {@link #NIO}. */
    39	    AUTO("auto");
    40	
    41	    private final String configValue;
    42	
    43	    SelectorImplementation(String configValue) {
    44	        this.configValue = configValue;
    45	    }
    46	
    47	    public String configValue() {
    48	        return configValue;
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    public static SelectorImplementation fromConfig(String value) {
    52	        if (value == null || value.trim().isEmpty()) {
    53	            throw new ConfigException(SocketServerConfigs.SOCKET_SELECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION_CONFIG,
    54	                value, "must be one of " + allowedValues());
    55	        }
    56	        String normalized = value.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
    57	        for (SelectorImplementation impl : values()) {
    58	            if (impl.configValue.equals(normalized)) {
    59	                return impl;
    60	            }
    61	        }
    62	        throw new ConfigException(SocketServerConfigs.SOCKET_SELECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION_CONFIG,
    63	            value, "must be one of " + allowedValues());
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    private static String allowedValues() {
    67	        return Arrays.stream(values())
    68	            .map(SelectorImplementation::configValue)
    69	            .collect(Collectors.joining(", "));
    70	    }
    71	}

   387	    /**
   388	     * Queue the given request for sending in the subsequent {@link #poll(long)} calls
   389	     * @param send The request to send
   390	     */
   391	    public void send(NetworkSend send) {
   392	        String connectionId = send.destinationId();
   393	        KafkaChannel channel = openOrClosingChannelOrFail(connectionId);
   394	        if (closingChannels.containsKey(connectionId)) {
   395	            // ensure notification via `disconnected`, leave channel in the state in which closing was triggered
   396	            this.failedSends.add(connectionId);
   397	        } else {
   398	            try {
   399	                channel.setSend(send);
   400	            } catch (Exception e) {
   401	                // update the state for consistency, the channel will be discarded after `close`
   402	                channel.state(ChannelState.FAILED_SEND);
   403	                // ensure notification via `disconnected` when `failedSends` are processed in the next poll
   404	                this.failedSends.add(connectionId);
   405	                close(channel, CloseMode.DISCARD_NO_NOTIFY);
   406	                if (!(e instanceof CancelledKeyException)) {
   407	                    log.error("Unexpected exception during send, closing connection {} and rethrowing exception.",
   408	                            connectionId, e);
   409	                    throw e;
   410	                }
   411	            }
   412	        }
   413	    }
   414	
   415	    /**
   416	     * Do whatever I/O can be done on each connection without blocking. This includes completing connections, completing
   417	     * disconnections, initiating new sends, or making progress on in-progress sends or receives.
   418	     *
   419	     * When this call is completed the user can check for completed sends, receives, connections or disconnects using
   420	     * {@link #completedSends()}, {@link #completedReceives()}, {@link #connected()}, {@link #disconnected()}. These
   421	     * lists will be cleared at the beginning of each `poll` call and repopulated by the call if there is
   422	     * any completed I/O.
   423	     *
   424	     * In the "Plaintext" setting, we are using socketChannel to read & write to the network. But for the "SSL" setting,
   425	     * we encrypt the data before we use socketChannel to write data to the network, and decrypt before we return the responses.
   426	     * This requires additional buffers to be maintained as we are reading from network, since the data on the wire is encrypted
   427	     * we won't be able to read exact no.of bytes as kafka protocol requires. We read as many bytes as we can, up to SSLEngine's
   428	     * application buffer size. This means we might be reading additional bytes than the requested size.
   429	     * If there is no further data to read from socketChannel selector won't invoke that channel and we have additional bytes
   430	     * in the buffer. To overcome this issue we added "keysWithBufferedRead" map which tracks channels which have data in the SSL
   431	     * buffers. If there are channels with buffered data that can by processed, we set "timeout" to 0 and process the data even
   432	     * if there is no more data to read from the socket.
   433	     *
   434	     * At most one entry is added to "completedReceives" for a channel in each poll. This is necessary to guarantee that
   435	     * requests from a channel are processed on the broker in the order they are sent. Since outstanding requests added
   436	     * by SocketServer to the request queue may be processed by different request handler threads, requests on each
   437	     * channel must be processed one-at-a-time to guarantee ordering.
   438	     *
   439	     * @param timeout The amount of time to wait, in milliseconds, which must be non-negative
   440	     * @throws IllegalArgumentException If `timeout` is negative
   441	     * @throws IllegalStateException If a send is given for which we have no existing connection or for which there is
   442	     *         already an in-progress send
   443	     */
   444	    @Override
   445	    public void poll(long timeout) throws IOException {
   446	        if (timeout < 0)
   447	            throw new IllegalArgumentException("timeout should be >= 0");
   448	
   449	        boolean madeReadProgressLastCall = madeReadProgressLastPoll;
   450	        clear();
   451	
   452	        boolean dataInBuffers = !keysWithBufferedRead.isEmpty();
   453	
   454	        if (!immediatelyConnectedKeys.isEmpty() || (madeReadProgressLastCall && dataInBuffers))
   455	            timeout = 0;
   456	
   457	        if (!memoryPool.isOutOfMemory() && outOfMemory) {
   458	            //we have recovered from memory pressure. unmute any channel not explicitly muted for other reasons
   459	            log.trace("Broker no longer low on memory - unmuting incoming sockets");
   460	            for (KafkaChannel channel : channels.values()) {
   461	                if (channel.isInMutableState() && !explicitlyMutedChannels.contains(channel)) {
   462	                    channel.maybeUnmute();
   463	                }
   464	            }
   465	            outOfMemory = false;
   466	        }
   467	
   468	        /* check ready keys */
   469	        long startSelect = time.nanoseconds();
   470	        int numReadyKeys = select(timeout);
   471	        long endSelect = time.nanoseconds();
   472	        this.sensors.selectTime.record(endSelect - startSelect, time.milliseconds(), false);
   473	
   474	        if (numReadyKeys > 0 || !immediatelyConnectedKeys.isEmpty() || dataInBuffers) {
   475	            Set<SelectionKey> readyKeys = this.nioSelector.selectedKeys();
   476	
   477	            // Poll from channels that have buffered data (but nothing more from the underlying socket)
   478	            if (dataInBuffers) {
   479	                keysWithBufferedRead.removeAll(readyKeys); //so no channel gets polled twice
   480	                Set<SelectionKey> toPoll = keysWithBufferedRead;
   481	                keysWithBufferedRead = new HashSet<>(); //poll() calls will repopulate if needed
   482	                pollSelectionKeys(toPoll, false, endSelect);
   483	            }
   484	
   485	            // Poll from channels where the underlying socket has more data
   486	            pollSelectionKeys(readyKeys, false, endSelect);
   487	            // Clear all selected keys so that they are excluded from the ready count for the next select
   488	            readyKeys.clear();
   489	
   490	            pollSelectionKeys(immediatelyConnectedKeys, true, endSelect);
   491	            immediatelyConnectedKeys.clear();
   492	        } else {
   493	            madeReadProgressLastPoll = true; //no work is also "progress"
   494	        }
   495	
   496	        long endIo = time.nanoseconds();
   497	        this.sensors.ioTime.record(endIo - endSelect, time.milliseconds(), false);
   498	
   499	        // Close channels that were delayed and are now ready to be closed
   500	        completeDelayedChannelClose(endIo);
   501	
   502	        // we use the time at the end of select to ensure that we don't close any connections that
   503	        // have just been processed in pollSelectionKeys
   504	        maybeCloseOldestConnection(endSelect);
   505	    }
   506	
   507	    /**
   508	     * handle any ready I/O on a set of selection keys
   509	     * @param selectionKeys set of keys to handle
   510	     * @param isImmediatelyConnected true if running over a set of keys for just-connected sockets
   511	     * @param currentTimeNanos time at which set of keys was determined
   512	     */
   513	    // package-private for testing
   514	    void pollSelectionKeys(Set<SelectionKey> selectionKeys,
   515	                           boolean isImmediatelyConnected,
   516	                           long currentTimeNanos) {

   518	            KafkaChannel channel = channel(key);
   519	            long channelStartTimeNanos = recordTimePerConnection ? time.nanoseconds() : 0;
   520	            boolean sendFailed = false;

   650	        long bytesSent = channel.write();
   651	        NetworkSend send = channel.maybeCompleteSend();
   652	        // We may complete the send with bytesSent < 1 if `TransportLayer.hasPendingWrites` was true and `channel.write()`
   653	        // caused the pending writes to be written to the socket channel buffer
   654	        if (bytesSent > 0 || send != null) {
   655	            long currentTimeMs = time.milliseconds();
   656	            if (bytesSent > 0)
   657	                this.sensors.recordBytesSent(nodeId, bytesSent, currentTimeMs);

   659	                this.completedSends.add(send);
   660	                this.sensors.recordCompletedSend(nodeId, send.size(), currentTimeMs);
   661	            }
   662	        }

   664	
   665	    private Collection<SelectionKey> determineHandlingOrder(Set<SelectionKey> selectionKeys) {
   666	        //it is possible that the iteration order over selectionKeys is the same every invocation.
   667	        //this may cause starvation of reads when memory is low. to address this we shuffle the keys if memory is low.
   668	        if (!outOfMemory && memoryPool.availableMemory() < lowMemThreshold) {
   669	            List<SelectionKey> shuffledKeys = new ArrayList<>(selectionKeys);
   670	            Collections.shuffle(shuffledKeys);
   671	            return shuffledKeys;
   672	        } else {
   673	            return selectionKeys;
   674	        }
   675	    }
   676	
   677	    private void attemptRead(KafkaChannel channel) throws IOException {
   678	        String nodeId = channel.id();
   679	
   680	        long bytesReceived = channel.read();
   681	        if (bytesReceived != 0) {
   682	            long currentTimeMs = time.milliseconds();
   683	            sensors.recordBytesReceived(nodeId, bytesReceived, currentTimeMs);
   684	            madeReadProgressLastPoll = true;
   685	
   686	            NetworkReceive receive = channel.maybeCompleteReceive();
   687	            if (receive != null) {
   688	                addToCompletedReceives(channel, receive, currentTimeMs);
   689	            }
   690	        }
   691	        if (channel.isMuted()) {
   692	            outOfMemory = true; //channel has muted itself due to memory pressure.
   693	        } else {
   694	            madeReadProgressLastPoll = true;
   695	        }
   696	    }
   697	
   698	    private boolean maybeReadFromClosingChannel(KafkaChannel channel) {
   699	        boolean hasPending;
   700	        if (channel.state().state() != ChannelState.State.READY)
   701	            hasPending = false;
   702	        else if (explicitlyMutedChannels.contains(channel) || hasCompletedReceive(channel))
   703	            hasPending = true;
   704	        else {
   705	            try {
   706	                attemptRead(channel);
   707	                hasPending = hasCompletedReceive(channel);
   708	            } catch (Exception e) {
   709	                log.trace("Read from closing channel failed, ignoring exception", e);
   710	                hasPending = false;
   711	            }
   712	        }
   713	        return hasPending;
   714	    }
   715	
   716	    // Record time spent in pollSelectionKeys for channel (moved into a method to keep checkstyle happy)
   717	    private void maybeRecordTimePerConnection(KafkaChannel channel, long startTimeNanos) {
   718	        if (recordTimePerConnection)

   813	    /**
   814	     * Clears completed receives. This is used by SocketServer to remove references to
   815	     * receive buffers after processing completed receives, without waiting for the next
   816	     * poll().
   817	     */
   818	    public void clearCompletedReceives() {
   819	        this.completedReceives.clear();
   820	    }
   821	
   822	    /**
   823	     * Clears completed sends. This is used by SocketServer to remove references to
   824	     * send buffers after processing completed sends, without waiting for the next
   825	     * poll().
   826	     */
   827	    public void clearCompletedSends() {
   828	        this.completedSends.clear();
   829	    }
   830	
   831	    /**
   832	     * Clears all the results from the previous poll. This is invoked by Selector at the start of
   833	     * a poll() when all the results from the previous poll are expected to have been handled.
   834	     * <p>
   835	     * SocketServer uses {@link #clearCompletedSends()} and {@link #clearCompletedReceives()} to
   836	     * clear `completedSends` and `completedReceives` as soon as they are processed to avoid
   837	     * holding onto large request/response buffers from multiple connections longer than necessary.
   838	     * Clients rely on Selector invoking {@link #clear()} at the start of each poll() since memory usage
   839	     * is less critical and clearing once-per-poll provides the flexibility to process these results in
   840	     * any order before the next poll.
   841	     */
   842	    private void clear() {
   843	        this.completedSends.clear();
   844	        this.completedReceives.clear();
   845	        this.connected.clear();
   846	        this.disconnected.clear();
   847	
   848	        // Remove closed channels after all their buffered receives have been processed or if a send was requested
   849	        for (Iterator<Map.Entry<String, KafkaChannel>> it = closingChannels.entrySet().iterator(); it.hasNext(); ) {
   850	            KafkaChannel channel = it.next().getValue();
   851	            boolean sendFailed = failedSends.remove(channel.id());
   852	            boolean hasPending = false;
   853	            if (!sendFailed)
   854	                hasPending = maybeReadFromClosingChannel(channel);
   855	            if (!hasPending) {
   856	                doClose(channel, true);
   857	                it.remove();
   858	            }
   859	        }
   860	
   861	        for (String channel : this.failedSends)
   862	            this.disconnected.put(channel, ChannelState.FAILED_SEND);
   863	        this.failedSends.clear();
   864	        this.madeReadProgressLastPoll = false;
   865	    }
   866	
   867	    /**
   868	     * Check for data, waiting up to the given timeout.
   869	     *
   870	     * @param timeoutMs Length of time to wait, in milliseconds, which must be non-negative
   871	     * @return The number of keys ready
   872	     */
   873	    private int select(long timeoutMs) throws IOException {
   874	        if (timeoutMs < 0L)
   875	            throw new IllegalArgumentException("timeout should be >= 0");
   876	
   877	        if (timeoutMs == 0L)
   878	            return this.nioSelector.selectNow();
   879	        else
   880	            return this.nioSelector.select(timeoutMs);
   881	    }
   882	
   883	    /**
   884	     * Close the connection identified by the given id
   885	     */
   886	    public void close(String id) {
   887	        KafkaChannel channel = this.channels.get(id);
   888	        if (channel != null) {
   889	            // There is no disconnect notification for local close, but updating
   890	            // channel state here anyway to avoid confusion.
   891	            channel.state(ChannelState.LOCAL_CLOSE);
   892	            close(channel, CloseMode.DISCARD_NO_NOTIFY);
   893	        } else {
   894	            KafkaChannel closingChannel = this.closingChannels.remove(id);
   895	            // Close any closing channel, leave the channel in the state in which closing was triggered
   896	            if (closingChannel != null)
   897	                doClose(closingChannel, false);
   898	        }
   899	    }
   900	
   901	    private void maybeDelayCloseOnAuthenticationFailure(KafkaChannel channel) {
   902	        DelayedAuthenticationFailureClose delayedClose = new DelayedAuthenticationFailureClose(channel, failedAuthenticationDelayMs);
   903	        if (delayedClosingChannels != null)
   904	            delayedClosingChannels.put(channel.id(), delayedClose);
   905	        else
   906	            delayedClose.closeNow();
   907	    }

   565	                        }
   566	                        log.debug("Successfully {}authenticated with {}", isReauthentication ?
   567	                            "re-" : "", channel.socketDescription());
   568	                    }
   569	                }
   570	                if (channel.ready() && channel.state() == ChannelState.NOT_CONNECTED)
   571	                    channel.state(ChannelState.READY);
   572	                Optional<NetworkReceive> responseReceivedDuringReauthentication = channel.pollResponseReceivedDuringReauthentication();
   573	                responseReceivedDuringReauthentication.ifPresent(receive -> {
   574	                    long currentTimeMs = time.milliseconds();
   575	                    addToCompletedReceives(channel, receive, currentTimeMs);
   576	                });

   578	                //if channel is ready and has bytes to read from socket or buffer, and has no
   579	                //previous completed receive then read from it
   580	                if (channel.ready() && (key.isReadable() || channel.hasBytesBuffered()) && !hasCompletedReceive(channel)
   581	                        && !explicitlyMutedChannels.contains(channel)) {
   582	                    attemptRead(channel);
   583	                }
   584	
   585	                if (channel.hasBytesBuffered() && !explicitlyMutedChannels.contains(channel)) {
   586	                    //this channel has bytes enqueued in intermediary buffers that we could not read
   587	                    //(possibly because no memory). it may be the case that the underlying socket will
   588	                    //not come up in the next poll() and so we need to remember this channel for the
   589	                    //next poll call otherwise data may be stuck in said buffers forever. If we attempt
   590	                    //to process buffered data and no progress is made, the channel buffered status is
   591	                    //cleared to avoid the overhead of checking every time.
   592	                    keysWithBufferedRead.add(key);
   593	                }
   594	
   595	                /* if channel is ready write to any sockets that have space in their buffer and for which we have data */
   596	
   597	                long nowNanos = channelStartTimeNanos != 0 ? channelStartTimeNanos : currentTimeNanos;
   598	                try {
   599	                    attemptWrite(key, channel, nowNanos);
   600	                } catch (Exception e) {
   601	                    sendFailed = true;
   602	                    throw e;
   603	                }
   604	
   605	                /* cancel any defunct sockets */
   606	                if (!key.isValid())
   607	                    close(channel, CloseMode.GRACEFUL);
   608	
   609	            } catch (Exception e) {
   610	                String desc = String.format("%s (channelId=%s)", channel.socketDescription(), channel.id());
   611	                if (e instanceof IOException) {
   612	                    log.debug("Connection with {} disconnected", desc, e);
   613	                } else if (e instanceof AuthenticationException) {
   614	                    boolean isReauthentication = channel.successfulAuthentications() > 0;
   615	                    if (isReauthentication)
   616	                        sensors.failedReauthentication.record();
   617	                    else
   618	                        sensors.failedAuthentication.record();
   619	                    String exceptionMessage = e.getMessage();
   620	                    if (e instanceof DelayedResponseAuthenticationException)
   621	                        exceptionMessage = e.getCause().getMessage();
   622	                    log.info("Failed {}authentication with {} ({})", isReauthentication ? "re-" : "",
   623	                        desc, exceptionMessage);
   624	                } else {
   625	                    log.warn("Unexpected error from {}; closing connection", desc, e);
   626	                }
   627	
   628	                if (e instanceof DelayedResponseAuthenticationException)
   629	                    maybeDelayCloseOnAuthenticationFailure(channel);
   630	                else
   631	                    close(channel, sendFailed ? CloseMode.NOTIFY_ONLY : CloseMode.GRACEFUL);
   632	            } finally {
   633	                maybeRecordTimePerConnection(channel, channelStartTimeNanos);
   634	            }
   635	        }
   636	    }

   638	    private void attemptWrite(SelectionKey key, KafkaChannel channel, long nowNanos) throws IOException {
   639	        if (channel.hasSend()
   640	                && channel.ready()
   641	                && key.isWritable()
   642	                && !channel.maybeBeginClientReauthentication(() -> nowNanos)) {
   643	            write(channel);
   644	        }
   645	    }

   957	    private void doClose(KafkaChannel channel, boolean notifyDisconnect) {
   958	        SelectionKey key = channel.selectionKey();
   959	        try {
   960	            immediatelyConnectedKeys.remove(key);
   961	            keysWithBufferedRead.remove(key);
   962	            channel.close();
   963	        } catch (IOException e) {
   964	            log.error("Exception closing connection to node {}:", channel.id(), e);
   965	        } finally {
   966	            key.cancel();
   967	            key.attach(null);
   968	        }
   969	
   970	        this.sensors.connectionClosed.record();
   971	        this.explicitlyMutedChannels.remove(channel);
   972	        if (notifyDisconnect)
   973	            this.disconnected.put(channel.id(), channel.state());
   974	    }

  1056	    private void addToCompletedReceives(KafkaChannel channel, NetworkReceive networkReceive, long currentTimeMs) {

   169	    /**
   170	     * Does handshake of transportLayer and authentication using configured authenticator.
   171	     * For SSL with client authentication enabled, {@link TransportLayer#handshake()} performs
   172	     * authentication. For SASL, authentication is performed by {@link Authenticator#authenticate()}.
   173	     */
   174	    public void prepare() throws AuthenticationException, IOException {
   175	        boolean authenticating = false;
   176	        try {
   177	            if (!transportLayer.ready())
   178	                transportLayer.handshake();
   179	            if (transportLayer.ready() && !authenticator.complete()) {
   180	                authenticating = true;
   181	                authenticator.authenticate();
   182	            }
   183	        } catch (AuthenticationException e) {
   184	            // Clients are notified of authentication exceptions to enable operations to be terminated
   185	            // without retries. Other errors are handled as network exceptions in Selector.
   186	            String remoteDesc = remoteAddress != null ? remoteAddress.toString() : null;
   187	            state = new ChannelState(ChannelState.State.AUTHENTICATION_FAILED, e, remoteDesc);
   188	            if (authenticating) {
   189	                delayCloseOnAuthenticationFailure();
   190	                throw new DelayedResponseAuthenticationException(e);
   191	            }
   192	            throw e;
   193	        }
   194	        if (ready()) {
   195	            ++successfulAuthentications;
   196	            state = ChannelState.READY;
   197	        }
   198	    }

   200	    public void disconnect() {
   201	        disconnected = true;
   202	        if (state == ChannelState.NOT_CONNECTED && remoteAddress != null) {
   203	            //if we captured the remote address we can provide more information
   204	            state = new ChannelState(ChannelState.State.NOT_CONNECTED, remoteAddress.toString());
   205	        }
   206	        transportLayer.disconnect();
   207	    }

   217	    public boolean finishConnect() throws IOException {
   218	        //we need to grab remoteAddr before finishConnect() is called otherwise
   219	        //it becomes inaccessible if the connection was refused.
   220	        SocketChannel socketChannel = transportLayer.socketChannel();
   221	        if (socketChannel != null) {
   222	            remoteAddress = socketChannel.getRemoteAddress();
   223	        }
   224	        boolean connected = transportLayer.finishConnect();
   225	        if (connected) {
   226	            if (ready()) {
   227	                state = ChannelState.READY;
   228	            } else if (remoteAddress != null) {
   229	                state = new ChannelState(ChannelState.State.AUTHENTICATE, remoteAddress.toString());
   230	            } else {
   231	                state = ChannelState.AUTHENTICATE;
   232	            }
   233	        }
   234	        return connected;
   235	    }

   250	     * externally muting a channel should be done via selector to ensure proper state handling
   251	     */
   252	    void mute() {
   253	        if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.NOT_MUTED) {
   254	            if (!disconnected) transportLayer.removeInterestOps(SelectionKey.OP_READ);
   255	            muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED;
   256	        }
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    /**
   260	     * Unmute the channel. The channel can be unmuted only if it is in the MUTED state. For other muted states
   261	     * (MUTED_AND_*), this is a no-op.
   262	     *
   263	     * @return Whether or not the channel is in the NOT_MUTED state after the call
   264	     */
   265	    boolean maybeUnmute() {
   266	        if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED) {
   267	            if (!disconnected) transportLayer.addInterestOps(SelectionKey.OP_READ);
   268	            muteState = ChannelMuteState.NOT_MUTED;
   269	        }
   270	        return muteState == ChannelMuteState.NOT_MUTED;
   271	    }
   272	
   273	    // Handle the specified channel mute-related event and transition the mute state according to the state machine.
   274	    public void handleChannelMuteEvent(ChannelMuteEvent event) {
   275	        boolean stateChanged = false;
   276	        switch (event) {
   277	            case REQUEST_RECEIVED:
   278	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED) {
   279	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING;
   280	                    stateChanged = true;
   281	                }
   282	                break;
   283	            case RESPONSE_SENT:
   284	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING) {
   285	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED;
   286	                    stateChanged = true;
   287	                }
   288	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING) {
   289	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED;
   290	                    stateChanged = true;
   291	                }
   292	                break;
   293	            case THROTTLE_STARTED:
   294	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING) {
   295	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING;
   296	                    stateChanged = true;
   297	                }
   298	                break;
   299	            case THROTTLE_ENDED:
   300	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED) {
   301	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED;
   302	                    stateChanged = true;
   303	                }
   304	                if (muteState == ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_THROTTLED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING) {
   305	                    muteState = ChannelMuteState.MUTED_AND_RESPONSE_PENDING;
   306	                    stateChanged = true;
   307	                }
   308	        }
   309	        if (!stateChanged) {
   310	            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot transition from " + muteState.name() + " for " + event.name());
   311	        }
   312	    }

   343	    public boolean isInMutableState() {
   344	        // Some requests do not require memory, so if we do not know what the current (or future) request is
   345	        // (receive == null) we don't mute. We also don't mute if whatever memory required has already been
   346	        // successfully allocated (if none is required for the currently-being-read request
   347	        // receive.memoryAllocated() is expected to return true)
   348	        if (receive == null || receive.memoryAllocated())
   349	            return false;
   350	        // also cannot mute if underlying transport is not in the ready state
   351	        return transportLayer.ready();
   352	    }

   396	    public NetworkSend maybeCompleteSend() {
   397	        if (send != null && send.completed()) {
   398	            midWrite = false;
   399	            transportLayer.removeInterestOps(SelectionKey.OP_WRITE);
   400	            NetworkSend result = send;
   401	            send = null;
   402	            return result;
   403	        }
   404	        return null;
   405	    }

   407	    public long read() throws IOException {
   408	        if (receive == null) {
   409	            receive = new NetworkReceive(maxReceiveSize, id, memoryPool);
   410	        }
   411	
   412	        long bytesReceived = receive(this.receive);
   413	
   414	        if (this.receive.requiredMemoryAmountKnown() && !this.receive.memoryAllocated() && isInMutableState()) {
   415	            //pool must be out of memory, mute ourselves.
   416	            mute();
   417	        }
   418	        return bytesReceived;
   419	    }

   426	        if (receive != null && receive.complete()) {
   427	            receive.payload().rewind();
   428	            NetworkReceive result = receive;
   429	            receive = null;
   430	            return result;
   431	        }
   432	        return null;

   460	    private long receive(NetworkReceive receive) throws IOException {
   461	        try {
   462	            return receive.readFrom(transportLayer);
   463	        } catch (SslAuthenticationException e) {
   464	            // With TLSv1.3, post-handshake messages may throw SSLExceptions, which are
   465	            // handled as authentication failures
   466	            String remoteDesc = remoteAddress != null ? remoteAddress.toString() : null;
   467	            state = new ChannelState(ChannelState.State.AUTHENTICATION_FAILED, e, remoteDesc);
   468	            throw e;
   469	        }
   470	    }

   520	     *            {@code SaslHandshakeRequest} that has been received on the server

   520	     *            {@code SaslHandshakeRequest} that has been received on the server
   521	     *            and that initiates re-authentication.
   522	     * @param nowNanosSupplier
   523	     *            {@code Supplier} of the current time. The value must be in
   524	     *            nanoseconds as per {@code System.nanoTime()} and is therefore only
   525	     *            useful when compared to such a value -- it's absolute value is
   526	     *            meaningless.
   527	     * 
   528	     * @return true if this is a server-side connection that has an expiration time
   529	     *         and at least 1 second has passed since the prior re-authentication
   530	     *         (if any) started to indicate that the re-authentication process has
   531	     *         begun, otherwise false
   532	     * @throws AuthenticationException
   533	     *             if re-authentication fails due to invalid credentials or other
   534	     *             security configuration errors
   535	     * @throws IOException
   536	     *             if read/write fails due to an I/O error
   537	     * @throws IllegalStateException
   538	     *             if this channel is not "ready"
   539	     */
   540	    public boolean maybeBeginServerReauthentication(NetworkReceive saslHandshakeNetworkReceive,
   541	            Supplier<Long> nowNanosSupplier) throws AuthenticationException, IOException {
   542	        if (!ready())
   543	            throw new IllegalStateException(
   544	                    "KafkaChannel should be \"ready\" when processing SASL Handshake for potential re-authentication");
   545	        /*
   546	         * Re-authentication is disabled if there is no session expiration time, in
   547	         * which case the SASL handshake network receive will be processed normally,
   548	         * which results in a failure result being sent to the client. Also, no need to
   549	         * check if we are muted since we are processing a received packet when we invoke
   550	         * this.
   551	         */
   552	        if (authenticator.serverSessionExpirationTimeNanos() == null)
   553	            return false;
   554	        /*
   555	         * We've delayed getting the time as long as possible in case we don't need it,
   556	         * but at this point we need it -- so get it now.
   557	         */
   558	        long nowNanos = nowNanosSupplier.get();
   559	        /*
   560	         * Cannot re-authenticate more than once every second; an attempt to do so will
   561	         * result in the SASL handshake network receive being processed normally, which
   562	         * results in a failure result being sent to the client.
   563	         */
   564	        if (lastReauthenticationStartNanos != 0
   565	                && nowNanos - lastReauthenticationStartNanos < MIN_REAUTH_INTERVAL_ONE_SECOND_NANOS)
   566	            return false;
   567	        lastReauthenticationStartNanos = nowNanos;
   568	        swapAuthenticatorsAndBeginReauthentication(
   569	                new ReauthenticationContext(authenticator, saslHandshakeNetworkReceive, nowNanos));
   570	        return true;
   571	    }

   573	    /**
   574	     * If this is a client-side connection that is not muted, there is no
   575	     * in-progress write, and there is a session expiration time defined that has
   576	     * past then begin the process of re-authenticating the connection and return
   577	     * true, otherwise return false
   578	     * 
   579	     * @param nowNanosSupplier
   580	     *            {@code Supplier} of the current time. The value must be in
   581	     *            nanoseconds as per {@code System.nanoTime()} and is therefore only
   582	     *            useful when compared to such a value -- it's absolute value is
   583	     *            meaningless.
   584	     * 
   585	     * @return true if this is a client-side connection that is not muted, there is
   586	     *         no in-progress write, and there is a session expiration time defined
   587	     *         that has past to indicate that the re-authentication process has
   588	     *         begun, otherwise false
   589	     * @throws AuthenticationException
   590	     *             if re-authentication fails due to invalid credentials or other
   591	     *             security configuration errors
   592	     * @throws IOException
   593	     *             if read/write fails due to an I/O error
   594	     * @throws IllegalStateException
   595	     *             if this channel is not "ready"
   596	     */
   597	    public boolean maybeBeginClientReauthentication(Supplier<Long> nowNanosSupplier)
   598	            throws AuthenticationException, IOException {
   599	        if (!ready())
   600	            throw new IllegalStateException(
   601	                    "KafkaChannel should always be \"ready\" when it is checked for possible re-authentication");
   602	        if (muteState != ChannelMuteState.NOT_MUTED || midWrite
   603	                || authenticator.clientSessionReauthenticationTimeNanos() == null)
   604	            return false;
   605	        /*
   606	         * We've delayed getting the time as long as possible in case we don't need it,
   607	         * but at this point we need it -- so get it now.
   608	         */
   609	        long nowNanos = nowNanosSupplier.get();
   610	        if (nowNanos < authenticator.clientSessionReauthenticationTimeNanos())
   611	            return false;
   612	        swapAuthenticatorsAndBeginReauthentication(new ReauthenticationContext(authenticator, receive, nowNanos));
   613	        receive = null;
   614	        return true;
   615	    }

   673	    private void swapAuthenticatorsAndBeginReauthentication(ReauthenticationContext reauthenticationContext)
   674	            throws IOException {
   675	        // it is up to the new authenticator to close the old one
   676	        // replace with a new one and begin the process of re-authenticating
   677	        authenticator = authenticatorCreator.get();
   678	        authenticator.reauthenticate(reauthenticationContext);
   679	    }

  1933	    override def reconfigurableConfigs(): util.Set[String] = {
  1934	      SocketServer.ListenerReconfigurableConfigs.asJava
  1935	    }
  1936	
  1937	    override def validateReconfiguration(configs: util.Map[String, _]): Unit = {
  1938	      val value = maxConnections(configs)
  1939	      if (value <= 0)
  1940	        throw new ConfigException(s"Invalid ${SocketServerConfigs.MAX_CONNECTIONS_CONFIG} $value")
  1941	
  1942	      val rate = maxConnectionCreationRate(configs)
  1943	      if (rate <= 0)
  1944	        throw new ConfigException(s"Invalid ${SocketServerConfigs.MAX_CONNECTION_CREATION_RATE_CONFIG} $rate")
  1945	    }
  1946	
  1947	    override def reconfigure(configs: util.Map[String, _]): Unit = {
  1948	      lock.synchronized {
  1949	        _maxConnections = maxConnections(configs)
  1950	        updateConnectionRateQuota(maxConnectionCreationRate(configs), ConnectionQuotaEntity.listenerQuotaEntity(listener.value))
  1951	        lock.notifyAll()
  1952	      }
  1953	    }
  1954	
  1955	    def close(): Unit = {
  1956	      metrics.removeSensor(connectionRateSensor.name)
  1957	      metrics.removeSensor(listenerConnectionRateThrottleSensor.name)
  1958	      metrics.removeSensor(ipConnectionRateThrottleSensor.name)
  1959	    }
  1960	
  1961	    private def maxConnections(configs: util.Map[String, _]): Int = {
  1962	      Option(configs.get(SocketServerConfigs.MAX_CONNECTIONS_CONFIG)).map(_.toString.toInt).getOrElse(Int.MaxValue)
  1963	    }
  1964	
  1965	    private def maxConnectionCreationRate(configs: util.Map[String, _]): Int = {
  1966	      Option(configs.get(SocketServerConfigs.MAX_CONNECTION_CREATION_RATE_CONFIG)).map(_.toString.toInt).getOrElse(Int.MaxValue)
  1967	    }
  1968	
  1969	    /**
  1970	     * Creates sensor for tracking the average throttle time on this listener due to hitting broker/listener connection
  1971	     * rate or IP connection rate quota. The average is out of all throttle times > 0, which is consistent with the
  1972	     * bandwidth and request quota throttle time metrics.
  1973	     */
  1974	    private def createConnectionRateThrottleSensor(throttlePrefix: String): Sensor = {
  1975	      val sensor = metrics.sensor(s"${throttlePrefix}ConnectionRateThrottleTime-${listener.value}")
  1976	      val metricName = metrics.metricName(s"${throttlePrefix}connection-accept-throttle-time",
  1977	        MetricsGroup,
  1978	        "Tracking average throttle-time, out of non-zero throttle times, per listener",
  1979	        Map(ListenerMetricTag -> listener.value).asJava)
  1980	      sensor.add(metricName, new Avg)
  1981	      sensor
  1982	    }
  1983	  }
  1984	
  1985	  /**
  1986	   * Close `channel` and decrement the connection count.
  1987	   */
  1988	  def closeChannel(log: Logging, listenerName: ListenerName, channel: SocketChannel): Unit = {
  1989	    if (channel != null) {
  1990	      log.debug(s"Closing connection from ${channel.socket.getRemoteSocketAddress}")
  1991	      dec(listenerName, channel.socket.getInetAddress)
  1992	      closeSocket(channel)
  1993	    }
  1994	  }

    34	public class PlaintextChannelBuilder implements ChannelBuilder {
    35	    private final ListenerName listenerName;
    36	    private Map<String, ?> configs;
    37	
    38	    /**
    39	     * Constructs a plaintext channel builder. ListenerName is non-null whenever
    40	     * it's instantiated in the broker and null otherwise.
    41	     */
    42	    public PlaintextChannelBuilder(ListenerName listenerName) {
    43	        this.listenerName = listenerName;
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    public void configure(Map<String, ?> configs) throws KafkaException {
    47	        this.configs = configs;
    48	    }
    49	
    50	    @Override
    51	    public KafkaChannel buildChannel(String id, SelectionKey key, int maxReceiveSize,
    52	                                     MemoryPool memoryPool, ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry) throws KafkaException {
    53	        PlaintextTransportLayer transportLayer = null;
    54	        try {
    55	            transportLayer = buildTransportLayer(key);
    56	            final PlaintextTransportLayer finalTransportLayer = transportLayer;
    57	            Supplier<Authenticator> authenticatorCreator = () -> new PlaintextAuthenticator(configs, finalTransportLayer, listenerName);
    58	            return buildChannel(id, transportLayer, authenticatorCreator, maxReceiveSize,
    59	                    memoryPool != null ? memoryPool : MemoryPool.NONE, metadataRegistry);
    60	        } catch (Exception e) {
    61	            // Ideally these resources are closed by the KafkaChannel but this builder should close the resources instead
    62	            // if an error occurs due to which KafkaChannel is not created.
    63	            Utils.closeQuietly(transportLayer, "transport layer for channel Id: " + id);
    64	            throw new KafkaException(e);
    65	        }
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    // visible for testing
    69	    KafkaChannel buildChannel(String id, TransportLayer transportLayer, Supplier<Authenticator> authenticatorCreator,
    70	                              int maxReceiveSize, MemoryPool memoryPool, ChannelMetadataRegistry metadataRegistry) {
    71	        return new KafkaChannel(id, transportLayer, authenticatorCreator, maxReceiveSize, memoryPool, metadataRegistry);
    72	    }

    87	            this.transportLayer = transportLayer;
    88	            this.principalBuilder = ChannelBuilders.createPrincipalBuilder(configs, null, null);
    89	            this.listenerName = listenerName;
    90	        }
    91	
    92	        @Override
    93	        public void authenticate() {}
    94	
    95	        @Override
    96	        public KafkaPrincipal principal() {
    97	            InetAddress clientAddress = transportLayer.socketChannel().socket().getInetAddress();
    98	            // listenerName should only be null in Client mode where principal() should not be called
    99	            if (listenerName == null)
   100	                throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected call to principal() when listenerName is null");
   101	            return principalBuilder.build(new PlaintextAuthenticationContext(clientAddress, listenerName.value()));
   102	        }
   103	
   104	        @Override
   105	        public Optional<KafkaPrincipalSerde> principalSerde() {
   106	            return principalBuilder instanceof KafkaPrincipalSerde ? Optional.of((KafkaPrincipalSerde) principalBuilder) : Optional.empty();
   107	        }
   108

   111	            return true;
   112	        }
   113

   116	            if (principalBuilder instanceof Closeable)
   117	                Utils.closeQuietly((Closeable) principalBuilder, "principal builder");
   118	        }

   109	
   110	    private static ChannelBuilder create(SecurityProtocol securityProtocol,
   111	                                         ConnectionMode connectionMode,
   112	                                         JaasContext.Type contextType,
   113	                                         AbstractConfig config,
   114	                                         ListenerName listenerName,
   115	                                         boolean isInterBrokerListener,
   116	                                         String clientSaslMechanism,
   117	                                         CredentialCache credentialCache,
   118	                                         DelegationTokenCache tokenCache,
   119	                                         Time time,
   120	                                         LogContext logContext,
   121	                                         Function<Short, ApiVersionsResponse> apiVersionSupplier) {
   122	        Map<String, Object> configs = channelBuilderConfigs(config, listenerName);
   123	
   124	        ChannelBuilder channelBuilder;
   125	        switch (securityProtocol) {
   126	            case SSL:
   127	                requireNonNullMode(connectionMode, securityProtocol);
   128	                channelBuilder = new SslChannelBuilder(connectionMode, listenerName, isInterBrokerListener, logContext);
   129	                break;
   130	            case SASL_SSL:
   131	            case SASL_PLAINTEXT:
   132	                requireNonNullMode(connectionMode, securityProtocol);
   133	                Map<String, JaasContext> jaasContexts;
   134	                String sslClientAuthOverride = null;
   135	                if (connectionMode == ConnectionMode.SERVER) {
   136	                    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
   137	                    List<String> enabledMechanisms = (List<String>) configs.get(BrokerSecurityConfigs.SASL_ENABLED_MECHANISMS_CONFIG);
   138	                    jaasContexts = new HashMap<>(enabledMechanisms.size());
   139	                    for (String mechanism : enabledMechanisms)
   140	                        jaasContexts.put(mechanism, JaasContext.loadServerContext(listenerName, mechanism, configs));
   141	
   142	                    // SSL client authentication is enabled in brokers for SASL_SSL only if listener-prefixed config is specified.
   143	                    if (listenerName != null && securityProtocol == SecurityProtocol.SASL_SSL) {
   144	                        String configuredClientAuth = (String) configs.get(BrokerSecurityConfigs.SSL_CLIENT_AUTH_CONFIG);
   145	                        String listenerClientAuth = (String) config.originalsWithPrefix(listenerName.configPrefix(), true)
   146	                                .get(BrokerSecurityConfigs.SSL_CLIENT_AUTH_CONFIG);
   147	
   148	                        // If `ssl.client.auth` is configured at the listener-level, we don't set an override and SslFactory
   149	                        // uses the value from `configs`. If not, we propagate `sslClientAuthOverride=NONE` to SslFactory and
   150	                        // it applies the override to the latest configs when it is configured or reconfigured. `Note that
   151	                        // ssl.client.auth` cannot be dynamically altered.
   152	                        if (listenerClientAuth == null) {
   153	                            sslClientAuthOverride = SslClientAuth.NONE.name().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
   154	                            if (configuredClientAuth != null && !configuredClientAuth.equalsIgnoreCase(SslClientAuth.NONE.name())) {
   155	                                log.warn("Broker configuration '{}' is applied only to SSL listeners. Listener-prefixed configuration can be used" +
   156	                                        " to enable SSL client authentication for SASL_SSL listeners. In future releases, broker-wide option without" +
   157	                                        " listener prefix may be applied to SASL_SSL listeners as well. All configuration options intended for specific" +
   158	                                        " listeners should be listener-prefixed.", BrokerSecurityConfigs.SSL_CLIENT_AUTH_CONFIG);
   159	                            }
   160	                        }
   161	                    }
   162	                } else {
   163	                    // Use server context for inter-broker client connections and client context for other clients
   164	                    JaasContext jaasContext = contextType == JaasContext.Type.CLIENT ? JaasContext.loadClientContext(configs) :
   165	                            JaasContext.loadServerContext(listenerName, clientSaslMechanism, configs);
   166	                    jaasContexts = Collections.singletonMap(clientSaslMechanism, jaasContext);
   167	                }
   168	                channelBuilder = new SaslChannelBuilder(connectionMode,
   169	                        jaasContexts,
   170	                        securityProtocol,
   171	                        listenerName,
   172	                        isInterBrokerListener,
   173	                        clientSaslMechanism,
   174	                        credentialCache,
   175	                        tokenCache,
   176	                        sslClientAuthOverride,
   177	                        time,
   178	                        logContext,
   179	                        apiVersionSupplier);
   180	                break;
   181	            case PLAINTEXT:
   182	                channelBuilder = new PlaintextChannelBuilder(listenerName);
   183	                break;
   184	            default:
   185	                throw new IllegalArgumentException("Unexpected securityProtocol " + securityProtocol);
   186	        }
   187	
   188	        channelBuilder.configure(configs);
   189	        return channelBuilder;
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    /**
   193	     * @return a mutable RecordingMap. The elements got from RecordingMap are marked as "used".
   194	     */
   195	    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
   196	    static Map<String, Object> channelBuilderConfigs(final AbstractConfig config, final ListenerName listenerName) {
   197	        Map<String, Object> parsedConfigs;
   198	        if (listenerName == null)
   199	            parsedConfigs = (Map<String, Object>) config.values();
   200	        else
   201	            parsedConfigs = config.valuesWithPrefixOverride(listenerName.configPrefix());
   202	
   203	        config.originals().entrySet().stream()
   204	            .filter(e -> !parsedConfigs.containsKey(e.getKey())) // exclude already parsed configs
   205	            // exclude already parsed listener prefix configs
   206	            .filter(e -> !(listenerName != null && e.getKey().startsWith(listenerName.configPrefix()) &&
   207	                parsedConfigs.containsKey(e.getKey().substring(listenerName.configPrefix().length()))))
   208	            // exclude keys like `{mechanism}.some.prop` if "listener.name." prefix is present and key `some.prop` exists in parsed configs.
   209	            .filter(e -> !(listenerName != null && parsedConfigs.containsKey(e.getKey().substring(e.getKey().indexOf('.') + 1))))
   210	            .forEach(e -> parsedConfigs.put(e.getKey(), e.getValue()));
   211	        return parsedConfigs;
   212	    }

   214	    private static void requireNonNullMode(ConnectionMode connectionMode, SecurityProtocol securityProtocol) {
   215	        if (connectionMode == null)
   216	            throw new IllegalArgumentException("`mode` must be non-null if `securityProtocol` is `" + securityProtocol + "`");
   217	    }

    17	package org.apache.kafka.common.network;
    18	
    19	import org.apache.kafka.common.memory.MemoryPool;
    20	
    21	import org.slf4j.Logger;
    22	import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
    23	
    24	import java.io.EOFException;
    25	import java.io.IOException;
    26	import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
    27	import java.nio.channels.ScatteringByteChannel;
    28	
    29	/**
    30	 * A size delimited Receive that consists of a 4 byte network-ordered size N followed by N bytes of content
    31	 */
    32	public class NetworkReceive implements Receive {
    33	
    34	    public static final String UNKNOWN_SOURCE = "";
    35	    public static final int UNLIMITED = -1;
    36	    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(NetworkReceive.class);
    37	    private static final ByteBuffer EMPTY_BUFFER = ByteBuffer.allocate(0);
    38	
    39	    private final String source;
    40	    private final ByteBuffer size;
    41	    private final int maxSize;
    42	    private final MemoryPool memoryPool;
    43	    private int requestedBufferSize = -1;
    44	    private ByteBuffer buffer;
    45	
    46	
    47	    public NetworkReceive(String source, ByteBuffer buffer) {
    48	        this(UNLIMITED, source);
    49	        this.buffer = buffer;
    50	    }
    51	
    52	    public NetworkReceive(String source) {
    53	        this(UNLIMITED, source);
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    public NetworkReceive(int maxSize, String source) {
    57	        this(maxSize, source, MemoryPool.NONE);
    58	    }
    59	
    60	    public NetworkReceive(int maxSize, String source, MemoryPool memoryPool) {
    61	        this.source = source;
    62	        this.size = ByteBuffer.allocate(4);
    63	        this.buffer = null;
    64	        this.maxSize = maxSize;
    65	        this.memoryPool = memoryPool;
    66	    }
    67	
    68	    public NetworkReceive() {
    69	        this(UNKNOWN_SOURCE);
    70	    }
    71	
    72	    @Override
    73	    public String source() {
    74	        return source;
    75	    }
    76	
    77	    @Override
    78	    public boolean complete() {
    79	        return !size.hasRemaining() && buffer != null && !buffer.hasRemaining();
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    public long readFrom(ScatteringByteChannel channel) throws IOException {
    83	        int read = 0;
    84	        if (size.hasRemaining()) {
    85	            int bytesRead = channel.read(size);
    86	            if (bytesRead < 0)
    87	                throw new EOFException();
    88	            read += bytesRead;
    89	            if (!size.hasRemaining()) {
    90	                size.rewind();
    91	                int receiveSize = size.getInt();
    92	                if (receiveSize < 0)
    93	                    throw new InvalidReceiveException("Invalid receive (size = " + receiveSize + ")");
    94	                if (maxSize != UNLIMITED && receiveSize > maxSize)
    95	                    throw new InvalidReceiveException("Invalid receive (size = " + receiveSize + " larger than " + maxSize + ")");
    96	                requestedBufferSize = receiveSize; // may be 0 for some payloads (SASL)
    97	                if (receiveSize == 0) {
    98	                    buffer = EMPTY_BUFFER;
    99	                }
   100	            }
   101	        }
   102	        if (buffer == null && requestedBufferSize != -1) { // we know the size we want but haven't been able to allocate it yet
   103	            buffer = memoryPool.tryAllocate(requestedBufferSize);
   104	            if (buffer == null)
   105	                log.trace("Broker low on memory - could not allocate buffer of size {} for source {}", requestedBufferSize, source);
   106	        }
   107	        if (buffer != null) {
   108	            int bytesRead = channel.read(buffer);
   109	            if (bytesRead < 0)
   110	                throw new EOFException();
   111	            read += bytesRead;
   112	        }
   113	
   114	        return read;
   115	    }
   116	
   117	    @Override
   118	    public boolean requiredMemoryAmountKnown() {
   119	        return requestedBufferSize != -1;
   120	    }
   121	
   122	    @Override
   123	    public boolean memoryAllocated() {
   124	        return buffer != null;
   125	    }
   126	
   127	
   128	    @Override
   129	    public void close() throws IOException {
   130	        if (buffer != null && buffer != EMPTY_BUFFER) {
   131	            memoryPool.release(buffer);
   132	            buffer = null;
   133	        }
   134	    }
   135	
   136	    public ByteBuffer payload() {
   137	        return this.buffer;
   138	    }
   139	
   140	    public int bytesRead() {
   141	        if (buffer == null)
   142	            return size.position();
   143	        return buffer.position() + size.position();
   144	    }
   145	
   146	    /**
   147	     * Returns the total size of the receive including payload and size buffer
   148	     * for use in metrics. This is consistent with {@link NetworkSend#size()}
   149	     */
   150	    public int size() {
   151	        return payload().limit() + size.limit();
   152	    }
   153	
   154	}

    17	package org.apache.kafka.common.network;
    18	
    19	import java.io.EOFException;
    20	import java.io.IOException;
    21	import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
    22	
    23	/**
    24	 * A send backed by an array of byte buffers
    25	 */
    26	public class ByteBufferSend implements Send {
    27	
    28	    private final long size;
    29	    protected final ByteBuffer[] buffers;
    30	    private long remaining;
    31	    private boolean pending = false;
    32	
    33	    public ByteBufferSend(ByteBuffer... buffers) {
    34	        this.buffers = buffers;
    35	        for (ByteBuffer buffer : buffers)
    36	            remaining += buffer.remaining();
    37	        this.size = remaining;
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    public ByteBufferSend(ByteBuffer[] buffers, long size) {
    41	        this.buffers = buffers;
    42	        this.size = size;
    43	        this.remaining = size;
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    @Override
    47	    public boolean completed() {
    48	        return remaining <= 0 && !pending;
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    @Override
    52	    public long size() {
    53	        return this.size;
    54	    }
    55	
    56	    @Override
    57	    public long writeTo(TransferableChannel channel) throws IOException {
    58	        long written = channel.write(buffers);
    59	        if (written < 0)
    60	            throw new EOFException("Wrote negative bytes to channel. This shouldn't happen.");
    61	        remaining -= written;
    62	        pending = channel.hasPendingWrites();
    63	        return written;
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    public long remaining() {
    67	        return remaining;
    68	    }
    69	
    70	    @Override
    71	    public String toString() {
    72	        return "ByteBufferSend(" +
    73	            ", size=" + size +
    74	            ", remaining=" + remaining +
    75	            ", pending=" + pending +
    76	            ')';
    77	    }
    78	
    79	    public static ByteBufferSend sizePrefixed(ByteBuffer buffer) {
    80	        ByteBuffer sizeBuffer = ByteBuffer.allocate(4);
    81	        sizeBuffer.putInt(0, buffer.remaining());
    82	        return new ByteBufferSend(sizeBuffer, buffer);
    83	    }
    84	}
